    f_fit, lev_fit = steady._tone_fit(tone_st, fs_st, 997.0)
    check("steady: bounded single-lobe tone fit returns true frequency and level",
          abs(f_fit - f_st) < 0.001 and abs(lev_fit + 23.86) < 0.02,
          f"f={f_fit:.6f} Hz (truth {f_st:.6f}); level={lev_fit:.3f} dBFS")

    # Long-record fit must not jump by 1/T to an adjacent correlation maximum.
    fs_long = 48000
    f_long = 997.092996
    t_long = np.arange(fs_long * 12, dtype=np.float64) / fs_long
    long_tone = 10 ** (-23.4 / 20) * np.sin(2 * np.pi * f_long * t_long + 0.37)
    f_long_fit, l_long_fit = steady._tone_fit(long_tone, fs_long, 997.0)
    check("steady: long tone frequency search remains on the central FFT lobe",
          abs(f_long_fit - f_long) < 0.001 and abs(l_long_fit + 23.4) < 0.02,
          f"f={f_long_fit:.6f} Hz (truth {f_long:.6f}); level={l_long_fit:.3f} dBFS")

    # Coherent projection reports the main tone and a known -60 dB cross-channel leak.
    t_map = np.arange(fs_st * 2, dtype=np.float64) / fs_st
    map_main = 10 ** (-23.4 / 20) * np.sin(2 * np.pi * f_st * t_map + 0.4)
    map_leak = 10 ** (-83.4 / 20) * np.sin(2 * np.pi * f_st * t_map - 0.2)
    main_level = steady._tone_level_at(map_main, fs_st, f_st)
    leak_level = steady._tone_level_at(map_leak, fs_st, f_st)
    check("steady: coherent L/R map quantifies a -60 dB cross-channel component",
          abs(main_level + 23.4) < 0.02 and abs((leak_level - main_level) + 60.0) < 0.02,
          f"main={main_level:.2f} dBFS; cross={leak_level:.2f} dBFS; Δ={leak_level-main_level:.2f} dB")

    # Residual analysis: a pure carrier + H2 should vanish from the non-harmonic residual,
    # while one deliberately injected broadband burst must be counted in exactly one window.
    fs_res = 48000
    f_res = 997.093
    t_res = np.arange(fs_res * 5, dtype=np.float64) / fs_res
    rng_res = np.random.default_rng(1919)
    base_res = rng_res.normal(0.0, 1e-5, t_res.size)
    amp_res = 10 ** (-23.4 / 20)
    for a_s, b_s in ((1.0, 3.0), (3.0, 5.0)):
        ix = (t_res >= a_s) & (t_res < b_s)
        base_res[ix] += amp_res * np.sin(2 * np.pi * f_res * t_res[ix])
        base_res[ix] += amp_res * 10 ** (-80 / 20) * np.sin(4 * np.pi * f_res * t_res[ix] + 0.3)
    burst_ix = (t_res >= 1.5) & (t_res < 1.6)
    base_res[burst_ix] += rng_res.normal(0.0, 1e-3, int(np.count_nonzero(burst_ix)))
    _rows, residual_stats = steady._analyse_channel(
        base_res, fs_res, {"idle_pre": (0.0, 1.0), "L_steady": (1.0, 3.0), "R_steady": (3.0, 5.0)},
        "L", f_res, -23.4, 0.1, 0.1, 20)
    stat_l = residual_stats["states"]["L_steady"]["residual_20_20k"]
    stat_r = residual_stats["states"]["R_steady"]["residual_20_20k"]
    check("steady: LS harmonic removal rejects tone leakage and detects a real broadband burst",
          stat_l["bursts"] == 1 and stat_r["bursts"] == 0 and stat_l["max"] > stat_l["median"] + 20,
          f"L burst windows={stat_l['bursts']}; R={stat_r['bursts']}; L median/max={stat_l['median']:.1f}/{stat_l['max']:.1f} dBFS")

    # The active playback L and R may have slightly different measured clock frequencies.
    _mapped_rows, mapped_stats = steady._analyse_channel(
        base_res, fs_res, {"idle_pre": (0.0, 1.0), "L_steady": (1.0, 3.0), "R_steady": (3.0, 5.0)},
        "L", {"L_steady": f_res, "R_steady": f_res + 0.02}, -23.4, 0.1, 0.1, 20)
    mapped_freqs = {r["state"]: r["projection_frequency_hz"] for r in _mapped_rows if r["state"] != "idle_pre"}
    check("steady: per-output measured frequency is used for each residual projection",
          abs(mapped_freqs["L_steady"] - f_res) < 1e-9 and abs(mapped_freqs["R_steady"] - (f_res + 0.02)) < 1e-9,
          f"L-state={mapped_freqs['L_steady']:.6f} Hz; R-state={mapped_freqs['R_steady']:.6f} Hz")

    # At 48 kHz there is no 40 kHz-to-Nyquist band. It must be N/A, not -300 dBFS.
    _rows_48, stats_48 = steady._analyse_channel(
        base_res, fs_res, {"idle_pre": (0.0, 1.0), "L_steady": (1.0, 3.0), "R_steady": (3.0, 5.0)},
        "L", f_res, -23.4, 0.1, 0.1, 20)
    hi_band_na = stats_48["states"]["L_steady"]["raw_40_nyquist"] is None
    check("steady: ultrasonic band below Nyquist is reported as N/A, not a false -300 dBFS",
          hi_band_na, f"raw 40k–Nyquist={stats_48['states']['L_steady']['raw_40_nyquist']}")

    # 0.1.10: high-frequency spectrum is broken into real bands clipped to Nyquist.
    bands_192 = steady._ultrasonic_band_edges(192000)
    bands_176 = steady._ultrasonic_band_edges(176400)
    bands_96 = steady._ultrasonic_band_edges(96000)
    check("steady: ultrasonic band map is clipped to the actual Nyquist frequency",
          [x[0] for x in bands_192] == ["40_50k", "50_60k", "60_70k", "70_80k", "80_90k", "90_96k"]
          and [x[0] for x in bands_176][-1] == "80_88p2k"
          and [x[0] for x in bands_96] == ["40_48k"],
          f"192k={[x[0] for x in bands_192]}; 176.4k={bands_176[-1][0]}; 96k={[x[0] for x in bands_96]}")

    # Integrated band level and ASD normalization must describe the same measured power.
    fs_band = 192000
    n_band = 19200
    t_band = np.arange(n_band, dtype=np.float64) / fs_band
    rng_band = np.random.default_rng(1910)
    x_band = rng_band.normal(0.0, 1e-4, n_band)
    win_band = np.ones(n_band, dtype=np.float64)
    f_band, p_band, df_band = steady._window_spectrum(x_band, fs_band, win_band)
    m_band = steady._band_metrics(f_band, p_band, df_band, 40000, 50000)
    expected_eq_asd = m_band["dbfs"] - 10.0 * math.log10(m_band["bandwidth_hz"])
    check("steady: ultrasonic band ASD normalization matches integrated band power",
          abs(m_band["equivalent_asd_dbfs_sqrt_hz"] - expected_eq_asd) < 1e-9
          and m_band["bins"] > 0,
          f"band={m_band['dbfs']:.2f} dBFS; equivalent ASD={m_band['equivalent_asd_dbfs_sqrt_hz']:.2f} dBFS/√Hz")

    # A narrow ultrasonic component must land in its own band, not be hidden in 40k-Nyquist.
    hf_t = np.arange(fs_band, dtype=np.float64) / fs_band
    hf = 10 ** (-40.0 / 20.0) * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * 45000.0 * hf_t)
    hf_win = np.ones(hf.size, dtype=np.float64)
    hf_f, hf_psd, hf_df = steady._window_spectrum(hf, fs_band, hf_win)
    hf_40_50 = steady._band_metrics(hf_f, hf_psd, hf_df, 40000, 50000)
    hf_50_60 = steady._band_metrics(hf_f, hf_psd, hf_df, 50000, 60000)
    check("steady: a 45-kHz line is isolated in the 40–50-kHz band",
          abs(hf_40_50["dbfs"] + 40.0) < 0.1 and hf_50_60["dbfs"] < -120.0,
          f"40–50k={hf_40_50['dbfs']:.2f} dBFS; 50–60k={hf_50_60['dbfs']:.1f} dBFS")

    # Baseline excursions are grouped into episodes, rather than reported only as window counts.
    burst_rows = [{"time_s": t, "residual_20_20k_dbfs": v}
                  for t, v in ((0.05, -90), (0.15, -89), (0.25, -88),
                              (0.55, -87), (0.75, -110))]
    burst_groups = steady._burst_episodes(burst_rows, -100.0, 0.1)
    check("steady: adjacent burst windows are grouped into physical episodes",
          burst_groups["windows"] == 4 and burst_groups["episodes"] == 2
          and abs(burst_groups["longest_s"] - 0.3) < 1e-9,
          f"windows={burst_groups['windows']}; episodes={burst_groups['episodes']}; longest={burst_groups['longest_s']:.2f}s")

    # Common-mode statistics must distinguish simultaneous from one-channel events.
    common_rows = []
    for tt, lv, rv in ((1.05, -90, -91), (1.15, -89, -90), (1.25, -90, -110),
                       (1.35, -110, -110), (1.45, -110, -90)):
        common_rows.extend((
            {"time_s": tt, "state": "L_steady", "channel": "L", "residual_20_20k_dbfs": lv},
            {"time_s": tt, "state": "L_steady", "channel": "R", "residual_20_20k_dbfs": rv},
        ))
    common_summary = {"L": {"baseline_dbfs": -110.0}, "R": {"baseline_dbfs": -110.0}}
    common_stats = steady._shared_excursion_stats(common_rows, common_summary, 0.1)["L_steady"]
    check("steady: simultaneous L/R excursions are counted separately",
          common_stats["both_excursion_windows"] == 2
          and common_stats["either_excursion_windows"] == 4
          and common_stats["shared_episodes"] == 1,
          f"both={common_stats['both_excursion_windows']}; either={common_stats['either_excursion_windows']}; shared episodes={common_stats['shared_episodes']}")

    # The new program must contain long pre/post silence while keeping total duration exact.
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="valvebench-steady-") as td:
        test_program = Path(td) / "test.raw"
        test_duration = steady._write_test_program(test_program, "S24_3LE", 48000, 997.0,
                                                   -18.0, duration_s=5, idle_s=3, gap_s=2)
        expected_duration = 2 * 5 + 2 * 3 + 2 + 4
        check("steady: long baseline test program has symmetric pre/post silence",
              abs(test_duration - expected_duration) < 1e-9
              and test_program.stat().st_size == int(expected_duration * 48000 * 2 * 3),
              f"duration={test_duration:.1f}s; expected={expected_duration}s; bytes={test_program.stat().st_size}")

    # A changed post-run idle floor must be visible as baseline drift.
    drift_fs = 48000
    drift_t = np.arange(drift_fs * 3, dtype=np.float64) / drift_fs
    drift_rng = np.random.default_rng(101010)
    drift_signal = drift_rng.normal(0.0, 1e-5, drift_t.size)
    post = drift_t >= 2.2
    drift_signal[post] = drift_rng.normal(0.0, 1e-4, np.count_nonzero(post))
    tone_mask = (drift_t >= 1.0) & (drift_t < 2.0)
    drift_signal[tone_mask] += 10 ** (-23.4 / 20) * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 997.0 * drift_t[tone_mask])
    _drift_rows, drift_stats = steady._analyse_channel(
        drift_signal, drift_fs,
        {"idle_pre": (0.0, 0.8), "L_steady": (1.0, 2.0), "idle_post": (2.2, 3.0)},
        "L", 997.0, -23.4, 0.1, 0.1, 20)
    check("steady: idle pre/post drift is reported instead of silently folding into baseline",
          drift_stats["baseline_stable"] is False and drift_stats["baseline_drift_db"] > 15.0,
          f"pre={drift_stats['baseline_pre_dbfs']:.2f}; post={drift_stats['baseline_post_dbfs']:.2f}; "
          f"drift={drift_stats['baseline_drift_db']:+.2f} dB")

    # Comparison command must preserve all source pairings rather than averaging unlike devices.
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="valvebench-steady-compare-") as td:
        p_json = Path(td) / "steady.json"
        sample = {"playback_device": "hw:3,0", "capture_device": "hw:2,0", "sample_rate": 192000,
                  "playback_format": "S24_3LE", "capture_format": "S24_3LE", "series_level_valid": True,
                  "channel_map": {"PLAY_L_only": {"cross_channel_leakage_db": -82.0},
                                  "PLAY_R_only": {"cross_channel_leakage_db": -83.0}},
                  "tone_continuity": {"L_steady": {"dropout_episodes": 1, "longest_dropout_ms": 80.0},
                                      "R_steady": {"dropout_episodes": 0, "longest_dropout_ms": 0.0}},
                  "analysis": {}}
        for ch in ("L", "R"):
            sample["analysis"][ch] = {"baseline_dbfs": -100.0, "states": {}}
            for state in ("L_steady", "R_steady"):
                sample["analysis"][ch]["states"][state] = {
                    "residual_20_20k": {"median": -95.0, "p95": -88.0, "bursts": 5, "episodes": 2},
                    "raw_40_nyquist": {"median": -78.0},
                }
        p_json.write_text(json.dumps(sample), encoding="utf-8")
        compare_text, compare_rows = steady.compare_steady_runs([p_json])
        check("steady-compare: report keeps explicit DAC→ADC pairing and metrics",
              len(compare_rows) == 1 and compare_rows[0]["playback"] == "hw:3,0"
              and compare_rows[0]["capture"] == "hw:2,0" and "hw:3,0 → hw:2,0" in compare_text
              and compare_rows[0]["L_carrier_dropout_episodes"] == 1
              and compare_rows[0]["R_carrier_dropout_episodes"] == 0,
              f"rows={len(compare_rows)}; row={compare_rows[0] if compare_rows else None}")

    # ALSA format probing preference/fallback is tested without real hardware.
    old_probe = steady._probe_play
    try:
        tried = []
        def fake_probe(dev, rate, fmt, channels=2):
            tried.append(fmt)
            return fmt == "S16_LE", "unsupported"
        steady._probe_play = fake_probe
        fmt_pick = steady.choose_device_format("hw:3,0", 192000, "playback")
    finally:
        steady._probe_play = old_probe
    check("steady: ALSA format negotiation falls back to a supported 16-bit mode",
          fmt_pick.fmt == "S16_LE" and tried == ["S24_3LE", "S24_LE", "S32_LE", "S16_LE"],
          f"selected={fmt_pick.fmt}; tried={tried}")

    # Playback-only and capture-only interfaces must remain visible for the
    # separate-DAC/separate-ADC workflow; do not reduce the list to duplex cards.
    old_parse_stream = backend_module.parse_stream
    try:
        backend_module.parse_stream = lambda card: {
            "Playback": [AltSet("S24_3LE", 2, (48000, 96000))],
            "Capture": [AltSet("S16_LE", 2, (48000,))],
        }
        play_rates = backend_module.supported_pcm_rates("hw:7,0", "Playback")
        cap_rates = backend_module.supported_pcm_rates("hw:7,0", "Capture")
        both_rates = backend_module.supported_duplex_rates("hw:7,0")
    finally:
        backend_module.parse_stream = old_parse_stream
    check("ALSA capabilities: separate playback/capture rates are reported independently",
          play_rates == (48000, 96000) and cap_rates == (48000,) and both_rates == (48000,),
          f"playback={play_rates}; capture={cap_rates}; duplex={both_rates}")

    # Strict A/B must also reject same-device passports with changed PCM formats.
    cfg_mismatch = {
        "time": "before", "label": "a", "device": "hw:2,0", "tone_hz": 997.0,
        "levels_dbfs": [-1.0], "repeats": 3, "silence_seconds": 4.0,
        "metadata": {"in_map": [0, 1], "out_map": [0, 1]},
        "rates": {"96000": {"io_config": {"device": "hw:2,0", "capture_format": "S24_3LE", "playback_format": "S24_3LE"}}},
    }
    cfg_mismatch_after = {**cfg_mismatch, "time": "after", "label": "b", "rates": {"96000": {"io_config": {
        "device": "hw:2,0", "capture_format": "S16_LE", "playback_format": "S24_3LE"}}}}
    format_mismatch = passport.passport_mismatches(cfg_mismatch, cfg_mismatch_after)
    text_mismatch = ""
    try:
        passport.compare_passports(cfg_mismatch, cfg_mismatch_after)
    except ValueError as exc:
        text_mismatch = str(exc)
    check("passport A/B: a changed PCM capture format is not silently compared",
          bool(format_mismatch) and "PCM-конфигурация" in text_mismatch,
          f"mismatches={format_mismatch}")

    # 0.1.11: carrier continuity is independent from the harmonic-residual detector.
    # A constant tone must not be declared interrupted.
    fs_car, dur_car, f_car = 48000, 1.0, 997.0
    t_car = np.arange(int(fs_car * dur_car), dtype=np.float64) / fs_car
    amp_car = 10 ** (-23.4 / 20)
    clean_carrier = amp_car * np.sin(2 * np.pi * f_car * t_car + 0.31)
    ref_car = steady._tone_level_at(clean_carrier, fs_car, f_car)
    clean_cont, _clean_track = steady._tone_continuity_track(
        clean_carrier, fs_car, "L_steady", "L", 0.0, dur_car, f_car, ref_car)
    check("steady: stable carrier has no dropout episodes",
          clean_cont["available"] and clean_cont["dropout_episodes"] == 0
          and clean_cont["longest_dropout_ms"] == 0.0
          and abs(clean_cont["median_tone_dbfs"] - ref_car) < 0.05,
          f"episodes={clean_cont['dropout_episodes']}; ref/median={ref_car:.2f}/{clean_cont['median_tone_dbfs']:.2f} dBFS")

    # A true 80-ms loss of carrier must be reported as one carrier dropout,
    # rather than just an additive residual burst.
    dropped_carrier = clean_carrier.copy()
    dropped_carrier[int(0.40 * fs_car):int(0.48 * fs_car)] = 0.0
    drop_cont, drop_track = steady._tone_continuity_track(
        dropped_carrier, fs_car, "L_steady", "L", 0.0, dur_car, f_car, ref_car)
    check("steady: 80-ms carrier dropout is detected independently of residual noise",
          drop_cont["dropout_episodes"] == 1 and 70.0 <= drop_cont["longest_dropout_ms"] <= 90.0
          and drop_cont["below_threshold_subwindows"] >= 7,
          f"episodes={drop_cont['dropout_episodes']}; longest={drop_cont['longest_dropout_ms']:.1f} ms; "
          f"low windows={drop_cont['below_threshold_subwindows']}")

    # An additive broadband disturbance can raise the residual while the
    # carrier remains present; it must not be confused with a dropout.
    rng_car = np.random.default_rng(11011)
    additive_carrier = clean_carrier.copy()
    additive_carrier[int(0.40 * fs_car):int(0.50 * fs_car)] += rng_car.normal(0.0, 0.015, int(0.10 * fs_car))
    additive_cont, _add_track = steady._tone_continuity_track(
        additive_carrier, fs_car, "L_steady", "L", 0.0, dur_car, f_car, ref_car)
    check("steady: additive noise burst is not classified as carrier dropout",
          additive_cont["dropout_episodes"] == 0,
          f"dropout episodes={additive_cont['dropout_episodes']}; minimum carrier={additive_cont['min_tone_dbfs']:.2f} dBFS")

    # Excluding a guard around active boundaries prevents fade-in/out windows
    # from leaking into the steady-state statistics.
    guarded = steady._guard_steady_ranges(
        {"idle_pre": (0.0, 2.0), "L_steady": (10.0, 50.0), "R_steady": (60.0, 100.0)}, 0.25)
    check("steady: analysis guard excludes active-state fade boundaries only",
          guarded["L_steady"] == (10.25, 49.75) and guarded["R_steady"] == (60.25, 99.75)
          and guarded["idle_pre"] == (0.0, 2.0),
          f"L={guarded['L_steady']}; R={guarded['R_steady']}; idle={guarded['idle_pre']}")

    state_ranges, guard_s = steady._build_steady_state_ranges(1.0, 15.0, 40.0, 8.0, 1.0, 0.1)
    check("steady: idle gap excludes the R fade-in and active ranges exclude both fades",
          abs(guard_s - 0.25) < 1e-12
          and state_ranges["L_steady"] == (17.25, 56.75)
          and state_ranges["idle_gap"] == (58.5, 65.5)
          and state_ranges["R_steady"] == (67.25, 106.75),
          f"guard={guard_s:.2f}s; L={state_ranges['L_steady']}; gap={state_ranges['idle_gap']}; R={state_ranges['R_steady']}")


    # 0.1.12-P0: потоковый декодер и WAV-писатель; память ограничена блоком.
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="valvebench-p0-") as td:
        td = Path(td)
        rng_p0 = np.random.default_rng(1204)
        n_p0 = 3 * 48000 * 2
        x_p0 = rng_p0.normal(0.0, 0.1, (n_p0 // 2, 2))
        raw_p0 = td / "cap.raw"
        raw_p0.write_bytes(to_pcm(x_p0, "S24_3LE"))
        reader_p0 = pcm_mod.StreamPcmReader(raw_p0, "S24_3LE", 2)
        full_p0 = pcm_mod.from_pcm(raw_p0.read_bytes(), "S24_3LE", 2)
        part_p0 = reader_p0.read_frames(1000, 120000)   # границы блоков внутри
        wav_p0 = td / "cap.wav"
        frames_p0 = pcm_mod.stream_to_float32_wav(raw_p0, "S24_3LE", 2, wav_p0, 48000)
        from scipy.io import wavfile as _wav
        fs_w, data_w = _wav.read(wav_p0)
        check("P0: StreamPcmReader.read_frames совпадает с from_pcm (включая границы блоков)",
              reader_p0.frames == n_p0 // 2
              and np.array_equal(part_p0, full_p0[1000:121000]),
              f"frames={reader_p0.frames}; макс. расхождение="
              f"{float(np.max(np.abs(part_p0 - full_p0[1000:121000]))):.2e}")
        check("P0: stream_to_float32_wav → scipy-читаемый float32 WAV со точными значениями",
              frames_p0 == n_p0 // 2 and fs_w == 48000 and data_w.dtype == np.float32
              and np.array_equal(data_w, full_p0.astype(np.float32)),
              f"frames={frames_p0}; dtype={data_w.dtype}; макс. расхождение с float32(decoded)="
              f"{float(np.max(np.abs(data_w - full_p0.astype(np.float32)))):.2e}")


    # 0.1.12-p1: XRUN-скан, классификация длины, onset по огибающей, мёртвая зона.
    big = "ok\n" + ("aplay: underrun!!! (at least 3.2 ms long)\n" * 4000)  # ~150 КБ
    sc = io_health_mod.scan_xrun_stderr(big)
    check("P1: XRUN-скан распознаёт underrun/overrun и не давится >64 КБ stderr",
          sc["underrun_count"] == 4000 and sc["count"] == 4000
          and len(sc["messages"]) == 20 and sc["messages_truncated"]
          and sc["stderr_bytes"] > 65536,
          f"underruns={sc['underrun_count']}; сохранено строк={len(sc['messages'])} "
          f"(лимит {sc['stderr_bytes']} B stderr)")
    sc2 = io_health_mod.scan_xrun_stderr("ok\narecord: overrun!!! (at least 1.1 ms long)\n")
    check("P1: overrun записи распознаётся отдельно",
          sc2["overrun_count"] == 1 and sc2["count"] == 1,
          f"overruns={sc2['overrun_count']}")
    ls = (io_health_mod.length_status(100, 100, 48000)["status"],
          io_health_mod.length_status(100 - 4800, 100, 48000)["status"],
          io_health_mod.length_status(100 + 4800, 100, 48000)["status"])
    check("P1: длина записи классифицируется ok/short/long",
          ls == ("ok", "short", "long"), f"статусы={ls}")
    # onset: синтетический сигнал с известным смещением +0.25 с (fade 1 с)
    fs_on = 48000
    dur_on = 10.0
    t_on = np.arange(int(fs_on * dur_on)) / fs_on
    tau_true = 0.25
    t0_exp = 4.0
    env_on = np.clip((t_on - (t0_exp + tau_true)) / 1.0, 0.0, 1.0)
    rng_on = np.random.default_rng(1408)
    sig_on = rng_on.normal(0, 1e-5, t_on.size) + 10 ** (-12 / 20) * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 997.0 * t_on) * env_on
    sig_st = np.column_stack((sig_on, sig_on))   # R = L: одинаковый onset
    on = io_health_mod.detect_playback_onset(sig_st, fs_on, 997.0,
                                             t0_exp, t0_exp, 1.0)
    check("P1: onset оценивается по форме огибающей (сдвиг +0.25 с восстанавливается)",
          on["reliable"] and on["applied"] and abs(on["deviation_s"] - tau_true) < 0.02,
          f"dev={on['deviation_s']:+.4f} с (истина +0.25); SNR L {on['confidence']['snr_db_L']:.0f} dB; "
          f"residual {on['confidence']['residual_fraction_L']:.3f}; applied={on['applied']}")
    # мёртвая зона: сдвиг 0.02 с не должен двигать диапазоны
    env_db = np.clip((t_on - (t0_exp + 0.02)) / 1.0, 0.0, 1.0)
    sig_db = rng_on.normal(0, 1e-5, t_on.size) + 10 ** (-12 / 20) * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 997.0 * t_on) * env_db
    on2 = io_health_mod.detect_playback_onset(np.column_stack((sig_db, sig_db)), fs_on, 997.0,
                                              t0_exp, t0_exp, 1.0)
    check("P1: мёртвая зона — смещение 0.02 с не применяется (метрики остаются прежними)",
          on2["reliable"] and not on2["applied"] and abs(on2["deviation_s"] - 0.02) < 0.02,
          f"dev={on2['deviation_s']:+.4f}; applied={on2['applied']} (ожидалось False)")
    # асимметрия раннего/позднего старта, граница поиска, None при неудаче
    def mk_onset(tau, with_tone=True):
        tt = np.arange(int(fs_on * 14.0)) / fs_on
        env = np.clip((tt - (t0_exp + tau)) / 1.0, 0.0, 1.0) if with_tone else np.zeros_like(tt)
        xx = rng_on.normal(0, 1e-5, tt.size) + 10 ** (-12 / 20) * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 997.0 * tt) * env
        return np.column_stack((xx, xx))
    ok_all = True
    det = []
    for tau, tone in ((-0.3, True), (0.3, True), (-0.8, True), (0.8, True), (1.2, True), (0.0, False)):
        on = io_health_mod.detect_playback_onset(mk_onset(tau, tone), fs_on, 997.0,
                                                 t0_exp, t0_exp, 1.0)
        if not tone:
            good = ((not on["reliable"]) and on["measured_onset_s"] is None
                    and on["confidence"]["snr_db_L"] is not None
                    and on["confidence"]["snr_db_L"] < 26.0)
        elif abs(tau) <= 1.0:
            good = (on["reliable"] and on["deviation_s"] is not None
                    and abs(on["deviation_s"] - tau) < 0.02
                    and on["measured_onset_s"] is not None
                    and not on["confidence"]["search_boundary_hit_L"])
        else:
            good = ((not on["reliable"]) and on["measured_onset_s"] is None
                    and on["confidence"]["search_boundary_hit_L"])
        ok_all &= good
        det.append(f"tau={tau:+.1f}{'' if tone else ' (без тона)'}: rel={on['reliable']} "
                   f"dev={on['deviation_s']} hit={on['confidence']['search_boundary_hit_L']}")
    xm = mk_onset(0.3)
    on_m = io_health_mod.detect_playback_onset(
        np.column_stack((mk_onset(0.3)[:, 0], mk_onset(0.5)[:, 0])), fs_on, 997.0,
        t0_exp, t0_exp, 1.0)
    ok_all &= (not on_m["reliable"]) and on_m["measured_onset_s"] is None
    det.append(f"L/R 0.3/0.5: rel={on_m['reliable']} (рассогласование)")
    # R-канал не подтверждён (L надёжен, R не обнаружен): диагностический статус,
    # основной алгоритм не меняется
    sig_lr = np.column_stack((mk_onset(0.3)[:, 0],
                              rng_on.normal(0, 0.5, int(fs_on * 14.0))))
    on_u = io_health_mod.detect_playback_onset(sig_lr, fs_on, 997.0, t0_exp, t0_exp, 1.0)
    on_s = io_health_mod.detect_playback_onset(mk_onset(0.3), fs_on, 997.0,
                                               t0_exp, t0_exp, 1.0)
    check("P1: right_channel_unverified — привязка только по L видна в JSON, алгоритм прежний",
          on_u["reliable"] and on_u["right_channel_unverified"] and on_u["applied"]
          and not on_s["right_channel_unverified"],
          f"L+шумный R: reliable={on_u['reliable']}, right_unverified="
          f"{on_u['right_channel_unverified']}, dev={on_u['deviation_s']:+.3f}; "
          f"симметричный случай: right_unverified={on_s['right_channel_unverified']}")
    check("P1: onset симметричен для раннего/позднего старта; граница поиска и отсутствие тона дают None",
          ok_all, " | ".join(det))

    # UI preference migration is one-time: update only old defaults, preserve user choices.
    h = lambda title: "pref_" + hashlib.sha1(str(title).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:12]
    old_ui = {
        "resp/" + h("Показывать"): 0,
        "resp/" + h("Генератор"): 0,
        "resp/" + h("Уровень @1 кГц, dBFS"): -20.0,
        "imd/" + h("Пик суммы, dBFS"): -12.0,
        "noise_response/" + h("График"): 0,
        "live/" + h("Уровень генератора, dBFS"): -20.0,
        "thd/" + h("Уровень генератора, dBFS"): -12.0,
        "live/" + h("Уровень теста, dBFS"): -30.0,
        "noise_response/" + h("Цифровой RMS-уровень, dBFS"): -42.0,
        "spectrum/pref_spectrum_fft": 0,
    }
    # Fix the helper to use the same hashlib function in a clean independent expression.
    old_ui = {k: v for k, v in old_ui.items()}
    # USB calibration identity survives port changes when VID:PID is unique,
    # but remains disambiguated by port for identical serial-less cards.
    usb_a = usb_profile_identity("0b05", "180c", "", "1-1", 1, 0)
    usb_b = usb_profile_identity("0B05", "180C", "", "1-2", 1, 0)
    usb_dup_a = usb_profile_identity("0b05", "180c", "", "1-1", 2, 0)
    usb_dup_b = usb_profile_identity("0b05", "180c", "", "1-2", 2, 0)
    check("Per-card calibration identity survives moving a unique USB card to another port",
          usb_a == usb_b and usb_dup_a != usb_dup_b,
          f"unique={usb_a == usb_b}; duplicate ports distinct={usb_dup_a != usb_dup_b}")

    migrated_ui = preferences.migrate_0_1_16_defaults(old_ui)
    migrated_again = preferences.migrate_0_1_16_defaults(migrated_ui)
    check("UI migration updates RIAA/low-level defaults once and preserves user choices",
          migrated_ui["resp/" + h("Показывать")] == 1
          and migrated_ui["noise_response/" + h("График")] == 1
          and migrated_ui["live/" + h("Уровень генератора, dBFS")] == -6
          and migrated_ui["thd/" + h("Уровень генератора, dBFS")] == -12.0
          and migrated_ui["live/" + h("Уровень теста, dBFS")] == -12
          and migrated_ui["noise_response/" + h("Желаемый RMS-уровень шума, dBFS")] == -12.0
          and migrated_ui["resp/" + h("Тестовый сигнал")] == 0
          and migrated_ui["resp/" + h("Уровень генератора @1 кГц, dBFS")] == -6.0
          and migrated_ui["imd/" + h("Пиковый уровень суммы двух тонов, dBFS")] == -6.0
          and migrated_ui["spectrum/pref_spectrum_fft"] == 5
          and migrated_again == migrated_ui,
          "старые значения по умолчанию исправлены; выбранные значения и повторный запуск миграции не изменяют настройки")
    user_ui = {"resp/" + h("Показывать"): 3, "live/" + h("Уровень генератора, dBFS"): -9.5}
    migrated_user = preferences.migrate_0_1_16_defaults(user_ui)
    check("UI migration does not overwrite non-default user selections",
          migrated_user["resp/" + h("Показывать")] == 3
          and migrated_user["live/" + h("Уровень генератора, dBFS")] == -9.5,
          f"RIAA view={migrated_user['resp/' + h('Показывать')]}; generator={migrated_user['live/' + h('Уровень генератора, dBFS')]} dBFS")

    # ---- 0.1.20: живые метрики и контроль целостности (valvebench/livemetrics.py)
    from . import livemetrics as lm
    from .backend import parse_alsa_log
    fs_l, n_l = 192000, 131072
    tt = np.arange(n_l) / fs_l
    rg = np.random.default_rng(2026)

    def _tone(h2=-80.0, noise=-110.0, f=1000.37):
        sig = dsp.dbfs_to_amp(noise) / math.sqrt(2) * math.sqrt(0.5 * fs_l / 20000.0)
        return (0.3 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * f * tt) + 0.3 * 10 ** (h2 / 20) * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 2 * f * tt + 1.0)
                + sig * rg.standard_normal(n_l))
    xt = _tone()
    r_live = lm.analyze_live_tone(xt, fs_l, 1000.0)
    check("Живая панель: тон вне сетки БПФ — уровень, H2 и шум в пределах 0.15 дБ / 1 дБ",
          r_live["ok"] and abs(r_live["level_dbfs"] - 20 * math.log10(0.3)) < 0.05
          and abs(r_live["harmonics"][0]["dbc"] + 80.0) < 0.15 and abs(r_live["noise_dbfs"] + 110.0) < 1.0,
          f"уровень {r_live['level_dbfs']:.3f}; H2 {r_live['harmonics'][0]['dbc']:.2f} dBc; N {r_live['noise_dbfs']:.2f} dBFS")
    r_nt = lm.analyze_live_tone(1e-6 * rg.standard_normal(n_l), fs_l, 1000.0)
    check("Живая панель: на шуме без тона честно «тона нет»", not r_nt["ok"], r_nt.get("reason", ""))
    z = xt.copy(); z[50000:50000 + 16500] = 0.0
    de = lm.detect_carrier_dropouts(z, fs_l, 1000.0)
    check("Провал несущей 86 мс найден с длительностью ±3 мс",
          len(de) == 1 and abs(de[0]["dur_s"] - 0.086) < 0.003,
          f"{[(round(e['start_s'], 3), round(e['dur_s'] * 1e3, 1)) for e in de]}")
    lost = np.concatenate([xt[:60000], xt[60000 + int(0.0505 * fs_l):]])[:110000]
    pj = lm.detect_phase_jumps(lost, fs_l, 1000.0)
    ci_lost = lm.frame_integrity(np.column_stack([lost, lost]), fs_l, 1000.0, (True, True))[0]
    check("Потеря отсчётов без обнуления: скачок фазы найден, окно забраковано, ложного «провала» нет",
          len(pj) == 1 and ci_lost.fatal and ci_lost.phase_jumps and not ci_lost.dropouts, f"{pj}")
    g1 = xt.copy(); g1[70000] += 1e-3
    gl = lm.detect_sample_glitches(g1, fs_l, 1000.0)
    check("Аномалия 1 отсчёта: найдена и классифицирована как single_sample",
          len(gl) == 1 and gl[0]["kind"] == "single_sample" and gl[0]["width"] == 1, f"{gl}")
    clean_fp = 0
    for k in range(10):
        rg2 = np.random.default_rng(900 + k)
        xx = _tone(h2=-60.0, noise=-100.0, f=float(rg2.uniform(300, 15000)))
        clean_fp += any(c.fatal for c in lm.frame_integrity(np.column_stack([xx, xx]), fs_l, None, (False, False)))
    check("Контроль целостности: 0 ложных срабатываний на 10 чистых окнах", clean_fp == 0, f"{clean_fp} ложных")
    quiet = 1e-6 * rg.standard_normal(n_l)
    ci = lm.frame_integrity(np.column_stack([xt, quiet]), fs_l, 1000.0, (True, True))
    check("Тихий канал не порождает ложных «остановок несущей»; в отчёте стоит пометка",
          not ci[1].dropouts and not ci[1].phase_jumps and bool(ci[1].note) and ci[0].driven, ci[1].note)
    ev_log, rest = parse_alsa_log("overrun!!! (at least 84.612 ms long)\nunderrun!!! (at least 960 frames long)\nover")
    ev_log2, rest2 = parse_alsa_log(rest + "run!!! (at least 12.5 ms long)\n")
    check("Разбор stderr ALSA: длительность в мс и кадрах, разорванная строка не теряется",
          len(ev_log) == 2 and abs(ev_log[0]["dur_ms"] - 84.612) < 1e-9 and ev_log[1]["dur_frames"] == 960.0
          and len(ev_log2) == 1 and abs(ev_log2[0]["dur_ms"] - 12.5) < 1e-9, f"{ev_log} / {ev_log2}")
    fw = np.arange(1, 20000) * 1.0
    fc_, pc_ = lm.octave_smooth(fw, np.ones_like(fw), 6)
    check("Сглаживание по долям октавы усредняет бины (белый шум остаётся белым)",
          np.allclose(pc_, 1.0) and fc_.size < 120, f"{fc_.size} полос")

    # ---- 0.1.20-dev2: замечания аудита и «АЧХ по пластинке»
    from .backend import xrun_suspect
    from . import peakhold as ph_mod
    tz = np.arange(131072) / 192000.0

    def _h2(f, h2=-40.0):
        return (0.3 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * f * tz + 0.4) + 0.3 * 10 ** (h2 / 20) * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 2 * f * tz + 1.0)
                + 1e-6 * rg.standard_normal(tz.size))
    r12 = lm.analyze_live_tone(_h2(12000.0), 192000, 12000.0)
    r10 = lm.analyze_live_tone(_h2(10000.0), 192000, 10000.0)
    check("THD: гармоник нет в полосе — «н/д», а не 0 %; H2 на границе полосы согласован с THD+N",
          r12["ok"] and r12["thd_na"] and r12["thd_pct"] is None
          and abs(r10["thd_pct"] - 1.0) < 0.02 and abs(r10["thdn_db"] + 40.0) < 0.2,
          f"12 кГц: n/a={r12['thd_na']}; 10 кГц: THD {r10['thd_pct']:.3f} %, THD+N {r10['thdn_db']:.2f} дБ")
    lv20 = [lm.analyze_live_tone(0.3 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 20.0 * tz + ph) + 1e-7 * rg.standard_normal(tz.size),
                                 192000, 20.0)["level_dbfs"] for ph in np.linspace(0, 2 * np.pi, 7)[:-1]]
    check("Уровень тона по МНК не зависит от фазы даже на 20 Гц (разброс < 0.001 дБ; проекция давала 0.14 дБ)",
          max(lv20) - min(lv20) < 1e-3 and abs(np.mean(lv20) - 20 * math.log10(0.3)) < 1e-3,
          f"размах {max(lv20) - min(lv20):.5f} дБ")
    a1, b1 = xrun_suspect("capture", 10 * 192000, 192000, 300.0)
    a2, b2 = xrun_suspect("playback", 10 * 192000, 192000, 2000.0)
    check("Интервалы XRUN растут с длительностью: capture 300 мс → +0.35 с, playback 2 с → −2.1…+3.2 с",
          b1 - 10 * 192000 == int(0.35 * 192000) and a2 == 10 * 192000 - int(2.1 * 192000)
          and b2 == 10 * 192000 + int(3.2 * 192000),
          f"capture 300 мс: [{(a1 - 10 * 192000) / 192000:+.2f}; {(b1 - 10 * 192000) / 192000:+.2f}] с; "
          f"playback 2 с: [{(a2 - 10 * 192000) / 192000:+.2f}; {(b2 - 10 * 192000) / 192000:+.2f}] с")
    fs_s, n_s = 48000, 16384
    ts_ = np.arange(int(12 * fs_s)) / fs_s
    k_ = 12000 / 100.0
    ph_s = 2 * np.pi * 100.0 * 12 / math.log(k_) * (k_ ** (ts_ / 12) - 1)
    fr_s = lambda f: -20.0 + 4.0 * np.log2(f / 1000.0)
    A_s = 10 ** (fr_s(100.0 * k_ ** (ts_ / 12)) / 20)
    Xs = np.column_stack([A_s * np.sin(ph_s), A_s * 10 ** (-45 / 20) * np.sin(ph_s + 0.4)
                          + 1e-6 * rg.standard_normal(ts_.size)])
    acc_s = ph_mod.PeakHoldAccumulator(6)
    for s0 in range(0, Xs.shape[0] - n_s, int(0.25 * fs_s)):
        rr = ph_mod.rta_job(Xs[s0:s0 + n_s], fs_s, n_s, 6)
        acc_s.update(rr["bands"], rr["tone"])
    th_ = acc_s.tone_hold()
    cs_ = acc_s.centers
    sl_ = (cs_ > 200) & (cs_ < 8000) & np.isfinite(th_[0])
    d_s, xt_s = ph_mod.split_driven(th_)
    check("АЧХ по пластинке (трекинг тона): свип «снят» с ошибкой ≤ 0.8 дБ, переходное затухание −45±2 дБ",
          sl_.sum() >= 15 and np.max(np.abs(th_[0][sl_] - fr_s(cs_[sl_]))) <= 0.8 and d_s == 0
          and abs(np.median(xt_s[sl_]) + 45.0) < 2.0,
          f"ошибка АЧХ {np.max(np.abs(th_[0][sl_] - fr_s(cs_[sl_]))):.2f} дБ; перекр. {np.median(xt_s[sl_]):.1f} дБ")
    ov_s = acc_s.snapshot("тест", ph_mod.PALETTE[0], "tone", fs_s)
    back_s = ph_mod.overlays_from_json(ph_mod.overlays_to_json([ov_s]))
    check("Сохранённые кривые переживают JSON (NaN ↔ null, цвет)",
          len(back_s) == 1 and np.allclose(back_s[0].levels, ov_s.levels, equal_nan=True, atol=1e-3),
          f"{len(back_s)} кривая, {int(np.isfinite(ov_s.levels).sum())} значений")

    # ---- 0.1.20-dev2: окно демодулятора — ровно целое число периодов даже при нецелом fs/f0
    fs97, n97 = 96000, 1 << 17
    t97 = np.arange(n97) / fs97
    x97 = 0.126 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 997.0 * t97) + 2e-7 * rg.standard_normal(n97)
    r97 = lm._Demod(x97, fs97, 997.0).residual()[2000:-2000]
    rms97 = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(r97 ** 2)))
    check("Остаток после вычитания несущей 997 Гц на 96 кГц лежит на уровне шума (раньше пульсация 2f₀ ≈ −88 dBFS)",
          rms97 < 6e-7, f"остаток {20 * math.log10(math.sqrt(2) * rms97):.1f} dBFS (шум −127 dBFS)")
    g97 = x97.copy()
    g97[60000] += 4e-4
    ev97 = lm.detect_sample_glitches(g97, fs97, 997.0)
    check("Сбой 1 отсчёт −68 dBFS на тоне 997 Гц найден ровно один раз (юбки демодулятора подавлены)",
          len(ev97) == 1 and ev97[0]["kind"] == "single_sample" and ev97[0]["sign"] == 1 and ev97[0]["index"] == 60000,
          f"{[(e['index'], e['kind']) for e in ev97]}")

    # ---- 0.1.20-dev3: порог детектора одиночных сбоев на длинном шуме (раньше «9σ» были ≈5.3σ)
    fp_total, fp_detail = 0, []
    rg3 = np.random.default_rng(303)
    for lsb in (2.5, 21.0, 100.0):
        cnt = 0
        for _ in range(4):
            xn = np.round(lsb * rg3.standard_normal(1_000_000)) * 2.0 ** -23
            cnt += len(lm.detect_sample_glitches(xn, 192000, None))
        fp_total += cnt
        fp_detail.append(f"{lsb:g} LSB: {cnt}")
    found12 = 0
    for _ in range(40):
        xs = 21 * 2.0 ** -23 * rg3.standard_normal(40000)
        xs[20000] += 12 * 21 * 2.0 ** -23
        found12 += any(abs(e["index"] - 20000) <= 1 for e in lm.detect_sample_glitches(xs, 192000, None))
    check("Детектор одиночных сбоев: 0 ложных срабатываний на 12 млн отсчётов шума (2.5/21/100 LSB), "
          "выброс 12σ шума находится в 100% случаев",
          fp_total == 0 and found12 == 40, "; ".join(fp_detail) + f"; найдено 12σ: {found12}/40")

    passed = sum(q["ok"] for q in checks)
    return {"passed": passed, "total": len(checks), "checks": checks}


def format_selftest(r):
    lines = [f"ValveBench self-test: {r['passed']}/{r['total']} PASS"]
    for q in r["checks"]:
        lines.append(f"{'PASS' if q['ok'] else 'FAIL'}  {q['name']}: {q['detail']}")
    return "\n".join(lines)


================================================================================
FILE: valvebench/signals.py
================================================================================

"""Тестовые сигналы: синусы, white/pink noise и inverse-RIAA shaped noise."""
from __future__ import annotations

import math
import numpy as np


def make_test_noise(fs: int, seconds: float, kind: str = "pink", rms_dbfs: float = -24.0,
                    f_lo: float = 10.0, f_hi: float = 20000.0, seed: int | None = 12345,
                    riaa_shape=None):
    """Вернуть (signal, info). RMS задан в dBFS по соглашению полного синуса.

    kind: white, pink или riaa. В riaa режиме шум предыскажён обратной
    playback-RIAA, чтобы выход исправного фонокорректора был приблизительно ровным.
    Пиковый запас ограничивается до -3 dBFS; фактический RMS отражается в info.
    """
    fs = int(fs)
    n = max(4096, int(round(float(seconds) * fs)))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    f = np.fft.rfftfreq(n, 1.0 / fs)
    z = rng.normal(size=f.size) + 1j * rng.normal(size=f.size)
    weight = np.ones_like(f)
    valid = (f >= max(1.0, f_lo)) & (f <= min(f_hi, 0.45 * fs))
    if kind == "pink":
        weight[valid] = 1.0 / np.sqrt(f[valid])
    elif kind == "riaa":
        if riaa_shape is None:
            raise ValueError("для riaa noise нужна функция обратной RIAA-кривой")
        weight[valid] = np.asarray(riaa_shape(f[valid]), dtype=float)
    elif kind != "white":
        raise ValueError(f"неизвестный тип шума: {kind}")
    weight[~valid] = 0.0
    spec = z * weight
    spec[0] = 0.0
    if n % 2 == 0:
        spec[-1] = spec[-1].real + 0j
    x = np.fft.irfft(spec, n=n)
    x -= np.mean(x)
    target_rms = 10.0 ** (float(rms_dbfs) / 20.0) / math.sqrt(2.0)
    rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(x * x)))
    if rms <= 0:
        raise ValueError("не удалось сформировать тестовый шум")
    x *= target_rms / rms
    peak_limit = 10.0 ** (-3.0 / 20.0)
    peak = float(np.max(np.abs(x)))
    scale_limited = peak > peak_limit
    if scale_limited:
        x *= peak_limit / peak
    actual_rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(x * x)))
    actual_dbfs = 20.0 * math.log10(max(actual_rms * math.sqrt(2.0), 1e-20))
    info = {
        "kind": kind, "fs": fs, "seconds": n / fs, "requested_rms_dbfs": float(rms_dbfs),
        "actual_rms_dbfs": actual_dbfs, "peak_dbfs": 20.0 * math.log10(max(float(np.max(np.abs(x))), 1e-20)),
        "peak_limited": scale_limited, "f_lo": float(f_lo), "f_hi": float(min(f_hi, 0.45 * fs)),
    }
    return x.astype(np.float64), info


def make_generator_signal(fs: int, waveform: str = "sine", frequency_hz: float = 1000.0,
                          level_dbfs: float = -6.0, seconds: float = 1.0,
                          noise_low_hz: float = 20.0, noise_high_hz: float = 20000.0,
                          seed: int = 12345):
    """Build a loopable two-purpose generator buffer and truthful level metadata.

    Periodic waveforms use a Nyquist-limited Fourier series (up to 99 harmonics)
    and are peak-normalized to the requested dBFS. Noise level is RMS dBFS and
    follows make_test_noise()'s -3 dBFS peak safety limit.
    """
    fs = int(fs)
    seconds = float(seconds)
    frequency_hz = float(frequency_hz)
    level_dbfs = float(level_dbfs)
    if fs < 8000 or not math.isfinite(seconds) or seconds <= 0:
        raise ValueError("нужна положительная длительность и Fs не ниже 8 кГц")
    if not math.isfinite(level_dbfs) or not -120.0 <= level_dbfs <= 0.0:
        raise ValueError("уровень генератора должен быть от -120 до 0 dBFS")
    n = max(4096, int(round(fs * seconds)))
    if waveform in ("white", "pink"):
        x, info = make_test_noise(fs, n / fs, kind=waveform, rms_dbfs=level_dbfs,
                                  f_lo=noise_low_hz, f_hi=noise_high_hz, seed=seed)
        info.update({"waveform": waveform, "frequency_hz": None,
                     "level_definition": "rms_dbfs"})
        return x, info

    if waveform not in ("sine", "triangle", "square", "sawtooth"):
        raise ValueError(f"неизвестная форма сигнала: {waveform}")
    if not math.isfinite(frequency_hz) or frequency_hz <= 0.0 or frequency_hz >= 0.45 * fs:
        raise ValueError(f"частота должна быть в пределах 0…{0.45 * fs:.0f} Гц")

    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / fs
    phase = 2.0 * np.pi * frequency_hz * t
    if waveform == "sine":
        x = np.sin(phase)
    else:
        # Keep the spectrum below Nyquist. The cap avoids expensive loops for
        # very low frequencies while preserving the visible shape well.
        hmax = min(99, int((0.45 * fs) // frequency_hz))
        if hmax < 1:
            raise ValueError("частота слишком высока для этой формы сигнала")
        x = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        if waveform in ("square", "triangle"):
            harmonics = range(1, hmax + 1, 2)
        else:
            harmonics = range(1, hmax + 1)
        for h in harmonics:
            if waveform == "square":
                coefficient = 1.0 / h
            elif waveform == "triangle":
                coefficient = ((-1.0) ** ((h - 1) // 2)) / (h * h)
            else:  # sawtooth
                coefficient = ((-1.0) ** (h + 1)) / h
            x += coefficient * np.sin(h * phase)
        peak = float(np.max(np.abs(x)))
        if peak <= 0 or not math.isfinite(peak):
            raise ValueError("не удалось сформировать периодический сигнал")
        x /= peak

    peak_target = 10.0 ** (level_dbfs / 20.0)
    x *= peak_target
    peak = float(np.max(np.abs(x)))
    rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(x * x)))
    info = {
        "waveform": waveform, "fs": fs, "seconds": n / fs,
        "frequency_hz": frequency_hz, "requested_level_dbfs": level_dbfs,
        "level_definition": "peak_dbfs", "peak_dbfs": 20.0 * math.log10(max(peak, 1e-30)),
        "rms_dbfs": 20.0 * math.log10(max(rms * math.sqrt(2.0), 1e-30)),
        "harmonics_generated": 1 if waveform == "sine" else hmax,
        "band_limit_hz": 0.45 * fs,
    }
    return x.astype(np.float64), info


================================================================================
FILE: valvebench/steady.py
================================================================================

"""Длительный matched-level тест ЦАП -> аналоговый тракт -> АЦП.

Отличие от прежних ad-hoc скриптов:
* отдельные playback и capture ALSA PCM (можно выбрать разные карты);
* проверка поддерживаемого PCM-формата до основного запуска;
* частота тона сначала ищется FFT, затем уточняется только в пределах половины
  интерполированного FFT-бина — без многолокального поиска по широкому интервалу;
* в каждом 100-мс окне DC и гармоники H1..Hn вычитаются LS-проекцией;
* правый и левый каналы анализируются одинаково;
* сохраняются summary, CSV и float WAV.

Это сравнительный инженерный тест, а не метрологическая сертификация.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import json
import math
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import tempfile
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
from scipy import fft as sp_fft
from scipy.optimize import minimize_scalar
from scipy.signal.windows import blackmanharris, hann

from .pcm import (FORMATS, to_pcm, from_pcm, stream_to_float32_wav,
                  FLOAT32_WAV_HEADER)
from . import io_health as io_health_mod


@dataclass
class DeviceFormat:
    device: str
    fmt: str
    channels: int = 2


def _run_probe(cmd, *, input_bytes=None, timeout=6.0):
    try:
        return subprocess.run(cmd, input=input_bytes, stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
                              stderr=subprocess.PIPE, timeout=timeout, check=False)
    except FileNotFoundError as exc:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Не найдена команда {cmd[0]!r}; установи alsa-utils") from exc
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as exc:
        # Для probe playback timeout означает, что ALSA открыл PCM, но тестовый
        # процесс не завершился штатно. Это не считается успешной проверкой.
        return subprocess.CompletedProcess(cmd, 124, b"", b"timeout")


def _probe_play(dev: str, fs: int, fmt: str, channels: int = 2) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    n = max(256, int(fs * 0.08))
    payload = to_pcm(np.zeros((n, channels), dtype=np.float64), fmt)
    cmd = ["aplay", "-q", "-D", dev, "-t", "raw", "-f", fmt,
           "-r", str(fs), "-c", str(channels), "-"]
    p = _run_probe(cmd, input_bytes=payload, timeout=5.0)
    return p.returncode == 0, (p.stderr or p.stdout).decode(errors="replace").strip()


def _probe_capture(dev: str, fs: int, fmt: str, channels: int = 2) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    cmd = ["arecord", "-q", "-D", dev, "-t", "raw", "-f", fmt,
           "-r", str(fs), "-c", str(channels), "-d", "1", "/dev/null"]
    p = _run_probe(cmd, timeout=5.0)
    return p.returncode == 0, (p.stderr or p.stdout).decode(errors="replace").strip()


def choose_device_format(dev: str, fs: int, direction: str, requested: str = "auto") -> DeviceFormat:
    """Find PCM format by actually opening the requested ALSA hw PCM.

    Preference is 24-bit packed, then 24-in-32, 32-bit, and 16-bit. For a
    requested exact format, only that format is tried.
    """
    if direction not in ("playback", "capture"):
        raise ValueError("direction must be playback or capture")
    candidates = [requested] if requested != "auto" else ["S24_3LE", "S24_LE", "S32_LE", "S16_LE"]
    errors = []
    for fmt in candidates:
        if fmt not in FORMATS:
            raise ValueError(f"Неизвестный PCM-формат: {fmt}")
        ok, detail = (_probe_play(dev, fs, fmt) if direction == "playback"
                      else _probe_capture(dev, fs, fmt))
        if ok:
            return DeviceFormat(dev, fmt, 2)
        errors.append(f"{fmt}: {detail or 'не поддерживается'}")
    raise RuntimeError(f"{dev}: нет подходящего формата {direction} при {fs} Гц. " + " | ".join(errors))


def _write_raw(path: Path, samples: np.ndarray, fmt: str) -> None:
    path.write_bytes(to_pcm(samples, fmt))


def _capture_and_play(play_file: Path, capture_file: Path, play: DeviceFormat,
                      capture: DeviceFormat, fs: int, capture_seconds: float,
                      lead_seconds: float = 0.5) -> dict:
    """Запуск arecord + aplay; 0.1.12-p1: stderr обоих процессов пишется во
    временные файлы (pipe исключён — любой объём сообщений не блокирует
    процессы). Возвращает пути stderr-файлов для XRUN-контроля; удаляет каталог
    вызывающий код после чтения.
    """
    err_dir = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="valvebench-io-"))
    rec_err_path = err_dir / "arecord.stderr"
    play_err_path = err_dir / "aplay.stderr"
    rec_cmd = ["arecord", "-q", "-D", capture.device, "-t", "raw", "-f", capture.fmt,
               "-r", str(fs), "-c", str(capture.channels), "-d",
               str(max(1, int(math.ceil(capture_seconds))),), str(capture_file)]
    with rec_err_path.open("w") as rec_err, play_err_path.open("w") as play_err:
        rec = subprocess.Popen(rec_cmd, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=rec_err, text=True)
        try:
            time.sleep(lead_seconds)
            play_cmd = ["aplay", "-q", "-D", play.device, "-t", "raw", "-f", play.fmt,
                        "-r", str(fs), "-c", str(play.channels), str(play_file)]
            p = subprocess.run(play_cmd, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=play_err,
                               timeout=capture_seconds + 10.0)
            if p.returncode:
                raise RuntimeError(f"Ошибка воспроизведения {play.device} ({play.fmt}): "
                                   + play_err_path.read_text(errors="replace")[-400:].strip())
            rec.communicate(timeout=capture_seconds + 10.0)
            if rec.returncode:
                raise RuntimeError(f"Ошибка записи {capture.device} ({capture.fmt}): "
                                   + rec_err_path.read_text(errors="replace")[-400:].strip())
        except Exception:
            if rec.poll() is None:
                rec.terminate()
                try:
                    rec.wait(timeout=2.0)
                except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                    rec.kill()
                    rec.wait(timeout=2.0)
            raise
    return {"capture_stderr": str(rec_err_path), "playback_stderr": str(play_err_path),
            "err_dir": str(err_dir)}


def _tone_chunk(start: int, count: int, fs: int, f: float, amplitude: float,
                left: bool, right: bool, fade: np.ndarray | None = None) -> np.ndarray:
    idx = np.arange(start, start + count, dtype=np.float64)
    mono = amplitude * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * idx / fs)
    if fade is not None:
        mono *= fade
    out = np.zeros((count, 2), dtype=np.float64)
    if left:
        out[:, 0] = mono
    if right:
        out[:, 1] = mono
    return out


def _write_test_program(path: Path, fmt: str, fs: int, f: float, level_dbfs: float,
                        duration_s: float = 40.0, idle_s: float = 15.0,
                        gap_s: float = 8.0, fade_s: float = 1.0) -> float:
    """Write a continuous L-only/R-only program with long digital-silence sections.

    Long pre/post idle periods make the noise baseline less vulnerable to rare
    events. The function returns the exact playback duration.
    """
    duration_s, idle_s, gap_s, fade_s = map(float, (duration_s, idle_s, gap_s, fade_s))
    if duration_s < 5 or idle_s < 3 or gap_s < 2 or fade_s < 0.1:
        raise ValueError("Требуются duration≥5 s, idle≥3 s, gap≥2 s, fade≥0.1 s")
    amp = 10.0 ** (level_dbfs / 20.0)
    l_fade_start = idle_s
    l_steady_start = l_fade_start + fade_s
    l_steady_end = l_steady_start + duration_s
    l_fade_end = l_steady_end + fade_s
    r_fade_start = l_fade_end + gap_s
    r_steady_start = r_fade_start + fade_s
    r_steady_end = r_steady_start + duration_s
    r_fade_end = r_steady_end + fade_s
    play_seconds = r_fade_end + idle_s
    total = int(round(play_seconds * fs))
    chunk = fs
    with path.open("wb") as fh:
        for start in range(0, total, chunk):
            count = min(chunk, total - start)
            local = np.arange(start, start + count, dtype=np.float64) / fs
            left = np.zeros(count, dtype=np.float64)
            right = np.zeros(count, dtype=np.float64)
            idx_l = (local >= l_fade_start) & (local < l_fade_end)
            idx_r = (local >= r_fade_start) & (local < r_fade_end)
            if np.any(idx_l):
                tt = local[idx_l]
                env = np.ones(tt.size, dtype=np.float64)
                fi = tt < l_steady_start
                fo = tt >= l_steady_end
                env[fi] = (tt[fi] - l_fade_start) / fade_s
                env[fo] = (l_fade_end - tt[fo]) / fade_s
                left[idx_l] = amp * env * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * tt)
            if np.any(idx_r):
                tt = local[idx_r]
                env = np.ones(tt.size, dtype=np.float64)
                fi = tt < r_steady_start
                fo = tt >= r_steady_end
                env[fi] = (tt[fi] - r_fade_start) / fade_s
                env[fo] = (r_fade_end - tt[fo]) / fade_s
                right[idx_r] = amp * env * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * tt)
            fh.write(to_pcm(np.column_stack((left, right)), fmt))
    return play_seconds


def _tone_fit(y: np.ndarray, fs: int, nominal_hz: float) -> tuple[float, float]:
    """Return (frequency Hz, sine peak dBFS) with a single-lobe bounded refinement.

    The old script optimized across nominal±0.5 Hz on a 40-s recording. That
    interval contains many correlation maxima spaced 1/T apart; bounded search
    could converge to a neighbouring lobe. Here the FFT peak is found first and
    refinement is confined to the central Hann lobe around that peak.
    """
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
    if y.ndim != 1 or len(y) < int(fs * 0.5):
        raise ValueError("Для оценки тона нужно не менее 0,5 с моно-записи")
    y = y - float(np.mean(y))
    n = len(y)
    w = hann(n, sym=False)
    # 8x zero-padding is enough to identify the main lobe; refine inside
    # a narrow interval that remains well inside its first nulls. This keeps
    # peak search memory moderate on older laptops at 192/384 kHz.
    nfft = sp_fft.next_fast_len(n * 8)
    mag = np.abs(sp_fft.rfft(y * w, n=nfft))
    freqs = sp_fft.rfftfreq(nfft, 1.0 / fs)
    search = (freqs >= nominal_hz - 5.0) & (freqs <= nominal_hz + 5.0)
    if not np.any(search):
        raise ValueError("частота тона вне диапазона поиска")
    indices = np.flatnonzero(search)
    peak_i = int(indices[np.argmax(mag[search])])
    guess = float(freqs[peak_i])
    refine_half_width = fs / nfft * 4.0
    lo, hi = max(nominal_hz - 5.0, guess - refine_half_width), min(nominal_hz + 5.0, guess + refine_half_width)
    # Four padded bins cover the FFT peak uncertainty while remaining inside
    # the Hann main lobe (first null is about 1/T from its centre).
    sample_idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    yy = y

    def fit_at(fq: float) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
        phase = 2.0 * np.pi * float(fq) * sample_idx / fs
        s = np.sin(phase)
        c = np.cos(phase)
        ss = float(np.dot(s, s)); cc = float(np.dot(c, c)); sc = float(np.dot(s, c))
        ys = float(np.dot(yy, s)); yc = float(np.dot(yy, c))
        det = ss * cc - sc * sc
        if det <= 1e-20:
            return 0.0, 0.0, 0.0
        a = (ys * cc - yc * sc) / det
        b = (yc * ss - ys * sc) / det
        return a, b, a * ys + b * yc

    def objective(fq: float) -> float:
        return -fit_at(fq)[2]

    opt = minimize_scalar(objective, bounds=(lo, hi), method="bounded",
                          options={"xatol": 1e-11, "maxiter": 80})
    f_est = float(opt.x)
    a, b, _ = fit_at(f_est)
    peak = math.hypot(a, b)
    return f_est, 20.0 * math.log10(max(peak, 1e-30))


def _tone_level_at(y: np.ndarray, fs: int, frequency_hz: float) -> float:
    """Coherent sine projection at a known frequency; used for channel mapping.

    This is not a broadband noise estimate. It answers how much of the known
    test tone is present in a given ADC channel.
    """
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
    y = y - float(np.mean(y))
    t = np.arange(y.size, dtype=np.float64) / fs
    phase = 2.0 * np.pi * float(frequency_hz) * t
    s, c = np.sin(phase), np.cos(phase)
    ss, cc, sc = float(s @ s), float(c @ c), float(s @ c)
    ys, yc = float(y @ s), float(y @ c)
    det = ss * cc - sc * sc
    if det <= 1e-20:
        return -300.0
    a = (ys * cc - yc * sc) / det
    b = (yc * ss - ys * sc) / det
    return 20.0 * math.log10(max(math.hypot(a, b), 1e-30))


def _guard_steady_ranges(state_ranges: dict[str, tuple[float, float]], guard_s: float) -> dict[str, tuple[float, float]]:
    """Move active analysis bounds away from the playback fade-in/fade-out edges.

    This does not shorten the generated test signal. It only excludes windows
    whose analysis aperture could overlap an envelope transition or timing
    uncertainty at the boundary.
    """
    guard_s = max(0.0, float(guard_s))
    guarded = dict(state_ranges)
    for state in ("L_steady", "R_steady"):
        if state not in guarded:
            continue
        start, end = guarded[state]
        if end - start <= 2.0 * guard_s:
            raise ValueError(f"Недостаточно длительный {state} после защитных интервалов")
        guarded[state] = (start + guard_s, end - guard_s)
    return guarded


def _build_steady_state_ranges(lead_s: float, idle_s: float, duration_s: float,
                               gap_s: float, fade_s: float, window_s: float) -> tuple[dict[str, tuple[float, float]], float]:
    """Build capture-time state ranges and guard active states from fade edges."""
    l_start = idle_s + fade_s
    l_end = l_start + duration_s
    gap_start = l_end + fade_s  # end of L fade-out
    r_fade_start = gap_start + gap_s
    r_start = r_fade_start + fade_s  # end of R fade-in
    r_end = r_start + duration_s
    post_start = r_end + fade_s
    ranges = {
        "idle_pre": (lead_s + 0.5, lead_s + idle_s - 0.5),
        "L_steady": (lead_s + l_start, lead_s + l_end),
        # Do not include the final fade-in of R in idle_gap.
        "idle_gap": (lead_s + gap_start + 0.5, lead_s + r_fade_start - 0.5),
        "R_steady": (lead_s + r_start, lead_s + r_end),
        "idle_post": (lead_s + post_start + 0.5, lead_s + post_start + idle_s - 0.5),
    }
    guard_s = max(0.25, min(1.0, window_s * 2.0))
    return _guard_steady_ranges(ranges, guard_s), guard_s


def _tone_continuity_track(y: np.ndarray, fs: int, state: str, channel: str,
                           start_s: float, end_s: float, frequency_hz: float,
                           reference_dbfs: float, subwindow_ms: float = 10.0,
                           threshold_below_db: float = 20.0,
                           min_episode_ms: float = 20.0) -> tuple[dict, list[dict]]:
    """Track presence of the expected carrier in short, contiguous subwindows.

    The residual/noise analysis removes H1..Hn and therefore cannot distinguish
    an additive disturbance from a missing carrier. This independent estimator
    fits only the fundamental in short windows and flags a dropout when it stays
    at least `threshold_below_db` below the measured steady carrier reference.
    """
    # 0.1.12-P0: y может быть memmap-колонкой float32 — вся запись в float64
    # не материализуется, окна конвертируются в _tone_level_at.
    if subwindow_ms <= 0 or threshold_below_db <= 0 or min_episode_ms <= 0:
        raise ValueError("Параметры контроля несущей должны быть положительными")
    # Keep at least five cycles in each fit. At the default 997 Hz this is 10 ms;
    # at low tone frequencies the window expands, up to 100 ms.
    sub_s = max(float(subwindow_ms) / 1000.0, 5.0 / float(frequency_hz))
    if sub_s > 0.100:
        return ({"available": False, "reason": "частота тона слишком низкая для короткого контроля несущей",
                 "subwindow_ms": sub_s * 1000.0, "reference_dbfs": float(reference_dbfs),
                 "dropout_episodes": 0, "total_dropout_ms": 0.0, "longest_dropout_ms": 0.0}, [])
    n = max(16, int(round(sub_s * fs)))
    actual_sub_ms = n / fs * 1000.0
    start = max(0, int(round(float(start_s) * fs)))
    end = min(y.size, int(round(float(end_s) * fs)))
    threshold_dbfs = float(reference_dbfs) - float(threshold_below_db)
    samples: list[dict] = []
    for off in range(start, max(start, end - n + 1), n):
        chunk = y[off:off + n]
        if chunk.size != n:
            continue
        level = _tone_level_at(chunk, fs, frequency_hz)
        start_time = off / fs
        samples.append({
            "start_s": start_time,
            "time_s": start_time + (n / fs) / 2.0,
            "state": state,
            "channel": channel,
            "tone_level_dbfs": float(level),
            "relative_to_reference_db": float(level - reference_dbfs),
            "below_threshold": bool(level <= threshold_dbfs),
            "dropout_episode_id": None,
        })

    # Group contiguous low-carrier subwindows; isolated one-window dips are
    # retained in the raw track but are not promoted to physical dropout events.
    low = [r for r in samples if r["below_threshold"]]
    groups: list[list[dict]] = []
    if low:
        current = [low[0]]
        step_s = n / fs
        for row in low[1:]:
            if row["start_s"] - current[-1]["start_s"] <= step_s * 1.51:
                current.append(row)
            else:
                groups.append(current)
                current = [row]
        groups.append(current)

    episodes = []
    for group in groups:
        duration_ms = len(group) * actual_sub_ms
        if duration_ms + 1e-6 < min_episode_ms:
            continue
        episode_id = len(episodes) + 1
        for row in group:
            row["dropout_episode_id"] = episode_id
        episodes.append({
            "id": episode_id,
            "start_s": float(group[0]["start_s"]),
            "end_s": float(group[-1]["start_s"] + n / fs),
            "duration_ms": float(duration_ms),
            "min_tone_dbfs": float(min(r["tone_level_dbfs"] for r in group)),
            "mean_tone_dbfs": float(np.mean([r["tone_level_dbfs"] for r in group])),
        })

    levels = np.asarray([r["tone_level_dbfs"] for r in samples], dtype=np.float64)
    confirmed_sample_count = sum(len(g) for g in groups if len(g) * actual_sub_ms + 1e-6 >= min_episode_ms)
    result = {
        "available": True,
        "channel": channel,
        "reference_dbfs": float(reference_dbfs),
        "threshold_dbfs": float(threshold_dbfs),
        "threshold_below_reference_db": float(threshold_below_db),
        "subwindow_ms": float(actual_sub_ms),
        "min_episode_ms": float(min_episode_ms),
        "subwindows": int(len(samples)),
        "median_tone_dbfs": float(np.median(levels)) if levels.size else None,
        "p01_tone_dbfs": float(np.percentile(levels, 1)) if levels.size else None,
        "min_tone_dbfs": float(np.min(levels)) if levels.size else None,
        "below_threshold_subwindows": int(sum(r["below_threshold"] for r in samples)),
        "confirmed_dropout_subwindows": int(confirmed_sample_count),
        "dropout_episodes": int(len(episodes)),
        "total_dropout_ms": float(sum(e["duration_ms"] for e in episodes)),
        "longest_dropout_ms": float(max((e["duration_ms"] for e in episodes), default=0.0)),
        "episodes": episodes,
    }
    return result, samples


def _calibration_trial(outdir: Path, trial: int, play: DeviceFormat, capture: DeviceFormat,
                       fs: int, tone_hz: float, tone_level: float) -> tuple[float, float]:
    play_file = outdir / f"calibration-{trial}.raw"
    cap_file = outdir / f"calibration-capture-{trial}.raw"
    n = int(round(2.5 * fs))
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / fs
    amp = 10.0 ** (tone_level / 20.0)
    signal = amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * tone_hz * t)
    _write_raw(play_file, np.column_stack((signal, signal)), play.fmt)
    try:
        _capture_and_play(play_file, cap_file, play, capture, fs, 3.5, lead_seconds=0.45)
        raw = cap_file.read_bytes()
        y = from_pcm(raw, capture.fmt, 2)
        # Stable one-second central section; avoid process start/stop transients.
        a = int(round(1.1 * fs)); b = int(round(2.1 * fs))
        if len(y) < b:
            raise RuntimeError("ALSA capture короче калибровочного участка")
        l = _tone_fit(y[a:b, 0], fs, tone_hz)[1]
        r = _tone_fit(y[a:b, 1], fs, tone_hz)[1]
        return l, r
    finally:
        for path in (play_file, cap_file):
            try:
                path.unlink()
            except OSError:
                pass


def _make_projection(n: int, fs: int, f0: float, max_harmonics: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """Orthonormal basis for per-window LS removal of DC and harmonic tones."""
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / fs
    cols = [np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)]
    max_h = min(int(max_harmonics), int(20000.0 // f0), int((fs / 2.0 * 0.98) // f0))
    for h in range(1, max_h + 1):
        ph = 2.0 * np.pi * h * f0 * t
        cols.extend((np.sin(ph), np.cos(ph)))
    design = np.column_stack(cols)
    q, _ = np.linalg.qr(design, mode="reduced")
    return q


def _ultrasonic_band_edges(fs: int) -> list[tuple[str, float, float]]:
    """Return fixed 10-kHz bands clipped to the actual Nyquist frequency."""
    nyquist = float(fs) / 2.0
    out = []
    def tag_khz(value: float) -> str:
        khz = value / 1000.0
        if math.isclose(khz, round(khz), rel_tol=0.0, abs_tol=1e-9):
            return str(int(round(khz)))
        return f"{khz:g}".replace(".", "p")
    for lo, nominal_hi in ((40000, 50000), (50000, 60000), (60000, 70000),
                           (70000, 80000), (80000, 90000), (90000, 96000)):
        hi = min(float(nominal_hi), nyquist)
        if hi > lo:
            label = f"{tag_khz(float(lo))}_{tag_khz(hi)}k"
            out.append((label, float(lo), hi))
    return out


def _window_spectrum(x: np.ndarray, fs: int, window: np.ndarray):
    """One-sided power spectral density in mean-square/Hz."""
    n = len(x)
    spec = np.fft.rfft(np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64) * window)
    psd = np.abs(spec) ** 2 / (fs * float(np.sum(window ** 2)))
    if n > 1:
        if n % 2 == 0:
            psd[1:-1] *= 2.0
        else:
            psd[1:] *= 2.0
    return np.fft.rfftfreq(n, 1.0 / fs), psd, fs / n


def _band_metrics(freqs: np.ndarray, psd: np.ndarray, df: float,
                  lo: float, hi: float) -> dict | None:
    """Integrated level plus median and 95th-percentile ASD in a band.

    ASD is expressed as dBFS/sqrt(Hz), with the same peak-sine dBFS
    convention used by the rest of ValveBench. The equivalent ASD is the
    integrated band level divided by the square root of the occupied bandwidth.
    """
    mask = (freqs >= lo) & (freqs < hi)
    if not np.any(mask) and math.isclose(hi, float(freqs[-1]), rel_tol=0.0, abs_tol=df * 0.51):
        mask = (freqs >= lo) & (freqs <= hi)
    if not np.any(mask):
        return None
    vals = np.maximum(psd[mask], 1e-300)
    width = float(np.count_nonzero(mask) * df)
    power = float(np.sum(vals) * df)
    band_dbfs = 10.0 * math.log10(max(2.0 * power, 1e-300))
    median_asd = 10.0 * math.log10(max(2.0 * float(np.median(vals)), 1e-300))
    p95_asd = 10.0 * math.log10(max(2.0 * float(np.percentile(vals, 95)), 1e-300))
    equiv_asd = band_dbfs - 10.0 * math.log10(max(width, 1e-300))
    return {"dbfs": band_dbfs, "asd_median_dbfs_sqrt_hz": median_asd,
            "asd_p95_dbfs_sqrt_hz": p95_asd,
            "equivalent_asd_dbfs_sqrt_hz": equiv_asd,
            "bins": int(np.count_nonzero(mask)), "bandwidth_hz": width}


def _band_dbfs(x: np.ndarray, fs: int, lo: float, hi: float, window: np.ndarray) -> float:
    """Compatibility helper used by tests and older report code."""
    freqs, psd, df = _window_spectrum(x, fs, window)
    metric = _band_metrics(freqs, psd, df, lo, min(hi, fs / 2.0))
    return float(metric["dbfs"]) if metric is not None else float("nan")


def _burst_episodes(rows: list[dict], threshold_dbfs: float, hop_s: float) -> dict:
    """Group adjacent threshold windows into episodes; windows are not events."""
    selected = sorted((r for r in rows if r["residual_20_20k_dbfs"] > threshold_dbfs),
                      key=lambda r: r["time_s"])
    if not selected:
        return {"windows": 0, "episodes": 0, "longest_s": 0.0, "peak_dbfs": None}
    groups = []
    current = [selected[0]]
    for row in selected[1:]:
        if row["time_s"] - current[-1]["time_s"] <= hop_s * 1.51:
            current.append(row)
        else:
            groups.append(current)
            current = [row]
    groups.append(current)
    return {"windows": len(selected), "episodes": len(groups),
            "longest_s": max(len(group) * hop_s for group in groups),
            "peak_dbfs": max(r["residual_20_20k_dbfs"] for r in selected)}


def _analyse_channel(y: np.ndarray, fs: int, state_ranges: dict[str, tuple[float, float]],
                     channel: str, f0: float | dict[str, float], target_dbfs: float, window_seconds: float,
                     hop_seconds: float, max_harmonics: int) -> tuple[list[dict], dict]:
    n = int(round(window_seconds * fs))
    hop = int(round(hop_seconds * fs))
    if n < 1024 or hop < 1:
        raise ValueError("Слишком короткое окно анализа")
    win = blackmanharris(n, sym=False)
    q_cache = {}
    out = []
    baseline_pre_values = []
    baseline_post_values = []
    band_edges = _ultrasonic_band_edges(fs)
    for state, (start_s, end_s) in state_ranges.items():
        start = int(round(start_s * fs)); end = min(len(y), int(round(end_s * fs)))
        for off in range(start, max(start, end - n + 1), hop):
            raw = np.asarray(y[off:off + n], dtype=np.float64)
            if raw.size != n:
                continue
            t_mid = (off + n / 2.0) / fs
            if state.startswith("idle"):
                residual = raw - float(np.mean(raw))
                removed_harmonics = 0
                f_state = None
            else:
                f_state = float(f0.get(state, f0.get("default"))) if isinstance(f0, dict) else float(f0)
                if f_state not in q_cache:
                    q_cache[f_state] = _make_projection(n, fs, f_state, max_harmonics)
                q = q_cache[f_state]
                coeff = q.T @ raw
                residual = raw - q @ coeff
                removed_harmonics = int((q.shape[1] - 1) // 2)
            lev20 = _band_dbfs(residual, fs, 20.0, 20000.0, win)
            freqs, psd, df = _window_spectrum(raw, fs, win)
            raw_bands = {label: _band_metrics(freqs, psd, df, lo, hi)
                         for label, lo, hi in band_edges}
            valid_band_power = [v["dbfs"] for v in raw_bands.values() if v is not None]
            lev40 = (10.0 * math.log10(sum(10.0 ** (v / 10.0) for v in valid_band_power))
                     if valid_band_power else None)
            row = {"time_s": t_mid, "state": state, "channel": channel,
                   "residual_20_20k_dbfs": lev20, "raw_40_nyquist_dbfs": lev40,
                   "raw_ultrasonic_bands": raw_bands,
                   "residual_removed_harmonics": removed_harmonics,
                   "projection_frequency_hz": f_state}
            out.append(row)
            if state == "idle_pre":
                baseline_pre_values.append(lev20)
            elif state == "idle_post":
                baseline_post_values.append(lev20)

    baseline_values = baseline_pre_values + baseline_post_values
    base_pre = float(np.median(baseline_pre_values)) if baseline_pre_values else float("nan")
    base_post = float(np.median(baseline_post_values)) if baseline_post_values else None
    base = float(np.median(baseline_values)) if baseline_values else float("nan")
    drift = (base_post - base_pre) if base_post is not None and math.isfinite(base_pre) else None
    idle_band_summary = {}
    for label, _lo, _hi in band_edges:
        idle_band_summary[label] = {}
        for idle_state in ("idle_pre", "idle_post"):
            idle_rows = [r for r in out if r["state"] == idle_state]
            band_values = [r["raw_ultrasonic_bands"].get(label) for r in idle_rows]
            band_values = [v for v in band_values if v is not None]
            idle_band_summary[label][idle_state] = (
                {"dbfs_median": float(np.median([v["dbfs"] for v in band_values])),
                 "asd_median_dbfs_sqrt_hz": float(np.median([v["asd_median_dbfs_sqrt_hz"] for v in band_values]))}
                if band_values else None)
    summary = {"baseline_dbfs": base, "baseline_pre_dbfs": base_pre,
               "baseline_post_dbfs": base_post, "baseline_drift_db": drift,
               "baseline_stable": (abs(drift) <= 3.0 if drift is not None else None),
               "idle_ultrasonic_bands": idle_band_summary, "states": {}}
    for state in ("L_steady", "R_steady"):
        vals = [r for r in out if r["state"] == state]
        v20 = np.asarray([r["residual_20_20k_dbfs"] for r in vals], dtype=float)
        v40_values = [r["raw_40_nyquist_dbfs"] for r in vals if r["raw_40_nyquist_dbfs"] is not None]
        v40 = np.asarray(v40_values, dtype=float)
        if not v20.size:
            continue
        burst_info = _burst_episodes(vals, base + 6.0, hop_seconds)
        band_summary = {}
        for label, _lo, _hi in band_edges:
            bvalues = [r["raw_ultrasonic_bands"].get(label) for r in vals]
            bvalues = [v for v in bvalues if v is not None]
            if not bvalues:
                band_summary[label] = None
                continue
            band_levels = np.asarray([v["dbfs"] for v in bvalues], dtype=float)
            asd_median = np.asarray([v["asd_median_dbfs_sqrt_hz"] for v in bvalues], dtype=float)
            asd_p95 = np.asarray([v["asd_p95_dbfs_sqrt_hz"] for v in bvalues], dtype=float)
            band_summary[label] = {
                "dbfs_median": float(np.median(band_levels)),
                "dbfs_p95": float(np.percentile(band_levels, 95)),
                "dbfs_max": float(np.max(band_levels)),
                "asd_median_dbfs_sqrt_hz": float(np.median(asd_median)),
                "asd_p95_dbfs_sqrt_hz": float(np.median(asd_p95)),
            }
        summary["states"][state] = {
            "windows": int(v20.size),
            "residual_20_20k": {"median": float(np.median(v20)), "p95": float(np.percentile(v20, 95)),
                                "max": float(np.max(v20)), "bursts": burst_info["windows"],
                                "episodes": burst_info["episodes"],
                                "longest_episode_s": burst_info["longest_s"],
                                "threshold_dbfs": base + 6.0},
            "raw_40_nyquist": ({"median": float(np.median(v40)), "p95": float(np.percentile(v40, 95)),
                                "max": float(np.max(v40))} if v40.size else None),
            "raw_ultrasonic_bands": band_summary,
        }
    return out, summary


def _shared_excursion_stats(rows: list[dict], summaries: dict, hop_s: float) -> dict:
    """Compare L/R residual excursions at matching times, state by state."""
    result = {}
    base_l = summaries["L"]["baseline_dbfs"]
    base_r = summaries["R"]["baseline_dbfs"]
    for state in ("L_steady", "R_steady"):
        by_channel = {
            ch: {round(r["time_s"], 6): r for r in rows
                 if r["state"] == state and r["channel"] == ch}
            for ch in ("L", "R")
        }
        common_times = sorted(set(by_channel["L"]) & set(by_channel["R"]))
        if not common_times:
            result[state] = {"windows": 0, "correlation_db": None,
                             "both_excursion_windows": 0, "either_excursion_windows": 0,
                             "shared_episodes": 0}
            continue
        lv = np.asarray([by_channel["L"][t]["residual_20_20k_dbfs"] for t in common_times])
        rv = np.asarray([by_channel["R"][t]["residual_20_20k_dbfs"] for t in common_times])
        corr = float(np.corrcoef(lv, rv)[0, 1]) if lv.size > 1 and np.std(lv) > 1e-12 and np.std(rv) > 1e-12 else None
        lm = lv > base_l + 6.0
        rm = rv > base_r + 6.0
        both = lm & rm
        either = lm | rm
        # Treat consecutive shared windows as one common-mode episode.
        shared_rows = [{"time_s": t, "residual_20_20k_dbfs": 1.0}
                       for t, flag in zip(common_times, both) if flag]
        shared = _burst_episodes(shared_rows, 0.0, hop_s)
        result[state] = {
            "windows": int(lv.size), "correlation_db": corr,
            "L_excursion_windows": int(np.count_nonzero(lm)),
            "R_excursion_windows": int(np.count_nonzero(rm)),
            "both_excursion_windows": int(np.count_nonzero(both)),
            "either_excursion_windows": int(np.count_nonzero(either)),
            "shared_episodes": int(shared["episodes"]),
        }
    return result


def compare_steady_runs(paths: list[str | Path]) -> tuple[str, list[dict]]:
    """Build one concise comparison table from previously saved steady.json files.

    Inputs may be JSON files or run directories. This does not claim that
    different physical pairings are the same instrument; the pair and settings
    remain explicit in every row.
    """
    rows = []
    for item in paths:
        path = Path(item)
        if path.is_dir():
            path = path / "steady.json"
        if not path.is_file():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Не найден steady.json: {path}")
        data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        channel_map = data.get("channel_map", {})
        analysis = data.get("analysis", {})
        l_map = channel_map.get("PLAY_L_only", {})
        r_map = channel_map.get("PLAY_R_only", {})
        l_state = analysis.get("L", {}).get("states", {})
        r_state = analysis.get("R", {}).get("states", {})
        l_on_l = l_state.get("L_steady", {})
        r_on_r = r_state.get("R_steady", {})
        l_res = l_on_l.get("residual_20_20k", {})
        r_res = r_on_r.get("residual_20_20k", {})
        l_ultra = l_on_l.get("raw_40_nyquist", {}) or {}
        r_ultra = r_on_r.get("raw_40_nyquist", {}) or {}
        continuity = data.get("tone_continuity", {})
        l_carrier = continuity.get("L_steady", {}) or {}
        r_carrier = continuity.get("R_steady", {}) or {}
        row = {
            "report": str(path),
            "playback": data.get("playback_device", "?"),
            "capture": data.get("capture_device", "?"),
            "playback_label": data.get("playback_label", ""),
            "capture_label": data.get("capture_label", ""),
            "sample_rate": data.get("sample_rate"),
            "playback_format": data.get("playback_format", "?"),
            "capture_format": data.get("capture_format", "?"),
            "baseline_L_dbfs": analysis.get("L", {}).get("baseline_dbfs"),
            "baseline_R_dbfs": analysis.get("R", {}).get("baseline_dbfs"),
            "L_to_R_crosstalk_dbc": l_map.get("cross_channel_leakage_db"),
            "R_to_L_crosstalk_dbc": r_map.get("cross_channel_leakage_db"),
            "L_residual_median_dbfs": l_res.get("median"),
            "L_residual_p95_dbfs": l_res.get("p95"),
            "L_burst_windows": l_res.get("bursts"),
            "L_burst_episodes": l_res.get("episodes"),
            "R_residual_median_dbfs": r_res.get("median"),
            "R_residual_p95_dbfs": r_res.get("p95"),
