            "R_burst_windows": r_res.get("bursts"),
            "R_burst_episodes": r_res.get("episodes"),
            "L_carrier_dropout_episodes": l_carrier.get("dropout_episodes"),
            "L_carrier_longest_dropout_ms": l_carrier.get("longest_dropout_ms"),
            "R_carrier_dropout_episodes": r_carrier.get("dropout_episodes"),
            "R_carrier_longest_dropout_ms": r_carrier.get("longest_dropout_ms"),
            "L_ultrasonic_40_nyquist_dbfs": l_ultra.get("median"),
            "R_ultrasonic_40_nyquist_dbfs": r_ultra.get("median"),
            "series_level_valid": data.get("series_level_valid"),
            "baseline_L_stable": analysis.get("L", {}).get("baseline_stable"),
            "baseline_R_stable": analysis.get("R", {}).get("baseline_stable"),
        }
        rows.append(row)
    headers = ("№", "ЦАП → АЦП", "Fs", "Шум тишины L/R", "Xtalk L→R / R→L",
               "Остаток L med/P95", "Остаток R med/P95", "Остаточные эпизоды L/R",
               "Carrier dropouts L/R", "40k–Nyq L/R")
    lines = ["СРАВНЕНИЕ ИЗМЕРЕНИЙ VALVEBENCH STEADY", "Разные пары устройств приведены как отдельные измерительные конфигурации.",
             " | ".join(headers), " | ".join(("---",) * len(headers))]
    for i, r in enumerate(rows, 1):
        def fmt(v, suffix=""):
            return "n/a" if v is None else f"{float(v):.1f}{suffix}"
        play_name = f"{r['playback_label']} [{r['playback']}]" if r["playback_label"] else r["playback"]
        capture_name = f"{r['capture_label']} [{r['capture']}]" if r["capture_label"] else r["capture"]
        pair = f"{play_name} → {capture_name}"
        lines.append(" | ".join((
            str(i), pair, str(r["sample_rate"]),
            f"{fmt(r['baseline_L_dbfs'])}/{fmt(r['baseline_R_dbfs'])}",
            f"{fmt(r['L_to_R_crosstalk_dbc'])}/{fmt(r['R_to_L_crosstalk_dbc'])}",
            f"{fmt(r['L_residual_median_dbfs'])}/{fmt(r['L_residual_p95_dbfs'])}",
            f"{fmt(r['R_residual_median_dbfs'])}/{fmt(r['R_residual_p95_dbfs'])}",
            f"{r['L_burst_episodes'] if r['L_burst_episodes'] is not None else 'n/a'}/"
            f"{r['R_burst_episodes'] if r['R_burst_episodes'] is not None else 'n/a'}",
            f"{(str(r['L_carrier_dropout_episodes']) + '/' + fmt(r['L_carrier_longest_dropout_ms'], 'ms')) if r['L_carrier_dropout_episodes'] is not None else 'n/a'}/"
            f"{(str(r['R_carrier_dropout_episodes']) + '/' + fmt(r['R_carrier_longest_dropout_ms'], 'ms')) if r['R_carrier_dropout_episodes'] is not None else 'n/a'}",
            f"{fmt(r['L_ultrasonic_40_nyquist_dbfs'])}/{fmt(r['R_ultrasonic_40_nyquist_dbfs'])}",
        )))
    return "\n".join(lines), rows


def _device_label(device: str) -> str:
    """Resolve a current ALSA card number to its current procfs label for the report."""
    match = re.match(r"^hw:(\d+)(?:,(\d+))?$", str(device))
    if not match:
        return str(device)
    card_no = int(match.group(1))
    try:
        from .backend import list_cards
        return next((name for number, name in list_cards() if number == card_no), f"ALSA card {card_no}")
    except Exception:
        return f"ALSA card {card_no}"


def _estimate_long_tone(y: np.ndarray, fs: int, nominal_hz: float, start_s: float, duration_s: float) -> tuple[float, float]:
    a = int(round(start_s * fs)); b = min(len(y), a + int(round(duration_s * fs)))
    if b - a < fs * 2:
        raise RuntimeError("Недостаточно данных для определения частоты тестового тона")
    return _tone_fit(y[a:b], fs, nominal_hz)


def run_steady_test(play_dev: str, rec_dev: str, fs: int = 192000, tone_hz: float = 997.0,
                    tone_level_dbfs: float = -18.0, target_dbfs: float = -23.4,
                    tolerance_db: float = 1.0, duration_s: float = 40.0,
                    window_s: float = 0.1, hop_s: float = 0.1, max_harmonics: int = 20,
                    idle_s: float = 15.0, gap_s: float = 8.0,
                    play_format: str = "auto", rec_format: str = "auto",
                    out_root: str | Path = "emu_measurements", input_fn=input,
                    print_fn=print) -> Path:
    """Interactive level match + sequential L/R steady-state measurement."""
    if fs < 48000 or fs > 384000:
        raise ValueError("Частота должна быть в диапазоне 48–384 кГц")
    if duration_s < 5 or duration_s > 300:
        raise ValueError("Длительность одного канала должна быть 5–300 секунд")
    if idle_s < 3 or idle_s > 120 or gap_s < 2 or gap_s > 60:
        raise ValueError("idle должен быть 3–120 с, gap — 2–60 с")
    if not (20.0 <= tone_hz <= min(20000.0, fs * 0.4)):
        raise ValueError("Частота тона вне допустимой полосы")
    stamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S")
    outdir = Path(out_root) / f"steady-{stamp}"
    outdir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    print_fn(f"Проверяю ALSA PCM: playback {play_dev}; capture {rec_dev}; {fs} Гц")
    play = choose_device_format(play_dev, fs, "playback", play_format)
    capture = choose_device_format(rec_dev, fs, "capture", rec_format)
    print_fn(f"Формат playback: {play.fmt}; формат capture: {capture.fmt}")
    print_fn(f"Цель: {target_dbfs:.2f} dBFS на каждом ADC; цифровой тон {tone_hz:g} Гц / {tone_level_dbfs:g} dBFS")
    print_fn("Подключение L→L, R→R. Для несбалансированных соединений используй соответствующие линейные входы; Phantom OFF.")
    trial = 1
    calibration_history = []
    while True:
        choice = input_fn("Enter — контроль уровня; q — выйти: ").strip().lower()
        if choice == "q":
            return outdir
        print_fn("  Записываю короткий контрольный тон…")
        l_db, r_db = _calibration_trial(outdir, trial, play, capture, fs, tone_hz, tone_level_dbfs)
        trial += 1
        dl, dr = l_db - target_dbfs, r_db - target_dbfs
        calibration_history.append({"trial": trial - 1, "adc_l_dbfs": l_db, "adc_r_dbfs": r_db,
                                    "delta_l_db": dl, "delta_r_db": dr})
        print_fn(f"  ADC L={l_db:.2f} dBFS ({dl:+.2f} дБ к цели) · ADC R={r_db:.2f} dBFS ({dr:+.2f} дБ к цели)")
        if abs(l_db - r_db) > 1.5:
            print_fn("  ПРЕДУПРЕЖДЕНИЕ: разница уровней L/R больше 1,5 дБ; общий регулятор не исправит этот дисбаланс.")
        if abs(dl) <= tolerance_db and abs(dr) <= tolerance_db:
            break
        avg = (dl + dr) / 2.0
        action = "УМЕНЬШИ" if avg > 0 else "УВЕЛИЧЬ"
        print_fn(f"  {action} выходной уровень/микшер примерно на {abs(avg):.1f} дБ и повтори.")
    choice = input_fn("Уровни согласованы. Не меняя громкость, Enter — основной тест; q — выход: ").strip().lower()
    if choice == "q":
        return outdir

    fade_s = 1.0
    play_seconds = 2.0 * duration_s + 2.0 * idle_s + gap_s + 4.0 * fade_s
    capture_seconds = play_seconds + 2.0
    play_file = outdir / "steady-playback.raw"
    capture_file = outdir / "steady-capture.raw"
    wav_file = outdir / "capture-float32.wav"
    csv_file = outdir / "timeline.csv"
    written_seconds = _write_test_program(play_file, play.fmt, fs, tone_hz, tone_level_dbfs,
                                          duration_s, idle_s, gap_s, fade_s)
    if abs(written_seconds - play_seconds) > 1.0 / fs:
        raise RuntimeError("Внутренняя ошибка расписания тестовой последовательности")
    print_fn(f"Основной тест: тишина {idle_s:.0f} с → L-only {duration_s:.0f} с → пауза {gap_s:.0f} с "
             f"→ R-only {duration_s:.0f} с → тишина {idle_s:.0f} с; запись около {capture_seconds:.0f} с.")
    lead_seconds = 1.0
    io_stderr = _capture_and_play(play_file, capture_file, play, capture, fs,
                                  capture_seconds, lead_seconds=lead_seconds)
    # 0.1.12-p1: XRUN-контроль по stderr обоих процессов
    io_health = {"xrun": io_health_mod.xrun_summary(
        Path(io_stderr["playback_stderr"]).read_text(errors="replace"),
        Path(io_stderr["capture_stderr"]).read_text(errors="replace"))}
    shutil.rmtree(io_stderr["err_dir"], ignore_errors=True)
    # 0.1.12-P0: capture декодируется потоково в float32 WAV, анализ читает окна
    # через memmap — пик памяти не зависит от длительности записи. Числа те же:
    # float32 точно хранит S24_3LE/S16_LE (мантисса 24 бита).
    frames = stream_to_float32_wav(capture_file, capture.fmt, capture.channels,
                                   wav_file, fs)
    raw = np.memmap(wav_file, dtype="<f4", mode="r", offset=FLOAT32_WAV_HEADER,
                    shape=(frames, capture.channels))
    # 0.1.12-p1: контроль фактической длины записи (жёсткий порог ниже остаётся)
    io_health["length"] = io_health_mod.length_status(
        frames, int(round(capture_seconds * fs)), fs)
    if raw.shape[0] < int(fs * (play_seconds + 1.0)):
        raise RuntimeError(f"Запись короче ожидаемой: {raw.shape[0] / fs:.2f} с")

    # Capture starts lead_seconds before playback. Exclude half-second margins
    # from idle states so USB/driver start/stop settling does not define baseline.
    # Capture-time ranges exclude fades from all idle/steady regions; active
    # ranges additionally receive a guard to absorb start-time uncertainty.
    ranges, transition_guard_s = _build_steady_state_ranges(
        lead_seconds, idle_s, duration_s, gap_s, fade_s, window_s)
    # 0.1.12-p1: фактическое начало воспроизведения по известной огибающей
    # (fade-in из _write_test_program), не порогом по амплитуде. Диапазоны
    # сдвигаются только при надёжном обнаружении и |сдвиг| > мёртвой зоны.
    t_onset_l = lead_seconds + idle_s
    t_onset_r = lead_seconds + idle_s + fade_s + duration_s + fade_s + gap_s
    onset = io_health_mod.detect_playback_onset(raw, fs, tone_hz, t_onset_l,
                                                t_onset_r, fade_s)
    io_health["onset"] = onset
    if onset["reliable"] and onset["applied"]:
        print_fn(f"  onset: измерено смещение запуска {onset['deviation_s']:+.3f} с; "
                 f"диапазоны состояний сдвинуты")
        ranges = {st: (a + onset["offset_s"], b + onset["offset_s"])
                  for st, (a, b) in ranges.items()}
    summaries = {}
    all_rows = []
    tone_levels = {}
    tone_map = {}
    f_by_state = {}
    # First estimate the active output frequency from its matching ADC channel.
    # Then project that same coherent frequency onto BOTH ADC channels so that
    # the report contains a real L/R channel map, not only the driven channel.
    for state, driven_idx, state_start in (("L_steady", 0, ranges["L_steady"][0]),
                                           ("R_steady", 1, ranges["R_steady"][0])):
        est_start = state_start + 0.5
        est_duration = min(3.0, duration_s - 1.0)
        f_est, _lev = _estimate_long_tone(raw[:, driven_idx], fs, tone_hz, est_start, est_duration)
        f_by_state[state] = f_est
        a = int(round(est_start * fs))
        b = min(len(raw), a + int(round(est_duration * fs)))
        map_levels = {
            "ADC_L": _tone_level_at(raw[a:b, 0], fs, f_est),
            "ADC_R": _tone_level_at(raw[a:b, 1], fs, f_est),
        }
        main_key = "ADC_L" if driven_idx == 0 else "ADC_R"
        leak_key = "ADC_R" if driven_idx == 0 else "ADC_L"
        main_level = map_levels[main_key]
        leak_level = map_levels[leak_key]
        measured_delta = main_level - target_dbfs
        within_target = abs(measured_delta) <= tolerance_db
        tone_map["PLAY_L_only" if state == "L_steady" else "PLAY_R_only"] = {
            "frequency_hz": f_est, "adc_levels_dbfs": map_levels,
            "cross_channel_leakage_db": leak_level - main_level,
            "main_level_delta_db": measured_delta, "main_level_within_target": within_target,
        }
        for idx, ch in ((0, "L"), (1, "R")):
            tone_levels[f"{state}_{ch}"] = {"frequency_hz": f_est, "level_dbfs": map_levels[f"ADC_{ch}"]}

    for idx, ch in ((0, "L"), (1, "R")):
        rows, stats = _analyse_channel(raw[:, idx], fs, ranges, ch, f_by_state, target_dbfs,
                                       window_s, hop_s, max_harmonics)
        all_rows.extend(rows)
        summaries[ch] = stats
    shared_stats = _shared_excursion_stats(all_rows, summaries, hop_s)
    tone_continuity = {}
    tone_continuity_rows = []
    for state, channel_idx, channel_name, map_key in (
        ("L_steady", 0, "L", "PLAY_L_only"),
        ("R_steady", 1, "R", "PLAY_R_only"),
    ):
        reference = tone_map[map_key]["adc_levels_dbfs"][f"ADC_{channel_name}"]
        continuity, continuity_rows = _tone_continuity_track(
            raw[:, channel_idx], fs, state, channel_name,
            ranges[state][0], ranges[state][1], f_by_state[state], reference,
            subwindow_ms=10.0, threshold_below_db=20.0, min_episode_ms=20.0,
        )
        tone_continuity[state] = continuity
        tone_continuity_rows.extend(continuity_rows)
    band_labels = [label for label, _lo, _hi in _ultrasonic_band_edges(fs)]
    csv_fields = ["time_s", "state", "channel", "residual_20_20k_dbfs", "raw_40_nyquist_dbfs",
                  "residual_removed_harmonics", "projection_frequency_hz"]
    for label in band_labels:
        csv_fields.extend((f"raw_{label}_dbfs", f"raw_{label}_asd_median_dbfs_sqrt_hz",
                           f"raw_{label}_asd_p95_dbfs_sqrt_hz"))
    with csv_file.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(fh, fieldnames=csv_fields)
        writer.writeheader()
        for row in all_rows:
            flat = {k: row.get(k) for k in csv_fields if k not in {f"raw_{b}_{s}" for b in band_labels for s in
                                                                    ("dbfs", "asd_median_dbfs_sqrt_hz", "asd_p95_dbfs_sqrt_hz")}}
            for label in band_labels:
                m = row.get("raw_ultrasonic_bands", {}).get(label)
                flat[f"raw_{label}_dbfs"] = m.get("dbfs") if m else None
                flat[f"raw_{label}_asd_median_dbfs_sqrt_hz"] = m.get("asd_median_dbfs_sqrt_hz") if m else None
                flat[f"raw_{label}_asd_p95_dbfs_sqrt_hz"] = m.get("asd_p95_dbfs_sqrt_hz") if m else None
            writer.writerow(flat)

    tone_csv_file = outdir / "tone-continuity.csv"
    tone_fields = ["start_s", "time_s", "state", "channel", "tone_level_dbfs",
                   "relative_to_reference_db", "below_threshold", "dropout_episode_id"]
    with tone_csv_file.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(fh, fieldnames=tone_fields)
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(tone_continuity_rows)

    # Remove the temporary raw data and playback file; WAV/CSV retain the evidence.
    for tmp in (play_file, capture_file):
        try:
            tmp.unlink()
        except OSError:
            pass
    report = []
    report.append("VALVEBENCH STEADY-STATE DAC → ADC REPORT")
    report.append(f"Playback: {play_dev} ({play.fmt}); capture: {rec_dev} ({capture.fmt})")
    report.append(f"Fs={fs} Hz · tone={tone_hz:g} Hz at {tone_level_dbfs:g} dBFS · target ADC={target_dbfs:g}±{tolerance_db:g} dBFS")
    report.append(f"Window={window_s*1000:g} ms · hop={hop_s*1000:g} ms · LS removal DC + H1..H{max_harmonics}; tone leakage is not measured as noise")
    report.append(f"Steady analysis edge guard={transition_guard_s*1000:.0f} ms at each active-state boundary; playback duration is unchanged")
    report.append("MEASURED CHANNEL MAP / TONE:")
    for key, val in tone_map.items():
        levels = val["adc_levels_dbfs"]
        status = "LEVEL OK" if val["main_level_within_target"] else "LEVEL MISMATCH — серия невалидна для matched-level A/B"
        report.append(f"  {key}: f={val['frequency_hz']:.6f} Hz; ADC L={levels['ADC_L']:.2f} dBFS; "
                      f"ADC R={levels['ADC_R']:.2f} dBFS; cross-channel={val['cross_channel_leakage_db']:.1f} dB; "
                      f"main Δtarget={val['main_level_delta_db']:+.2f} dB [{status}]")
    # 0.1.12-p1: валидность серии = уровни ∧ нет XRUN ∧ длина в допуске ∧
    # надёжное выравнивание; причины невалидности перечисляются отдельно.
    validity_reasons = []
    for key, val in tone_map.items():
        if not val["main_level_within_target"]:
            validity_reasons.append(f"{key}: уровень {val['main_level_delta_db']:+.2f} dB от цели")
    xr = io_health["xrun"]
    if xr["total_xruns"]:
        validity_reasons.append(
            f"XRUN: playback underruns {xr['playback']['count']}, "
            f"capture overruns {xr['capture']['count']}")
    ln = io_health["length"]
    if ln["status"] != "ok":
        validity_reasons.append(f"длина записи {ln['status']}: "
                                f"{ln['deviation_ms']:+.1f} ms ({ln['deviation_frames']:+d} кадров)")
    if not io_health["onset"]["reliable"]:
        validity_reasons.append(f"выравнивание ненадёжно: {io_health['onset'].get('reason')}")
    series_valid = not validity_reasons
    report.append("SERIES LEVEL VALIDITY: " + ("OK" if series_valid else
                  "INVALID — " + "; ".join(validity_reasons)))
    report.append("INPUT/OUTPUT HEALTH (io_health):")
    report.append(f"  XRUN: playback underruns={xr['playback']['count']}, "
                  f"capture overruns={xr['capture']['count']} "
                  f"(stderr aplay {xr['playback']['stderr_bytes']} B, "
                  f"arecord {xr['capture']['stderr_bytes']} B); {xr['note']}")
    report.append(f"  length: {ln['status']} (отклонение {ln['deviation_ms']:+.1f} ms / "
                  f"{ln['deviation_frames']:+d} кадров от {ln['expected_frames']}; "
                  f"допуск ±{ln['tolerance_ms']:.0f} ms)")
    on = io_health["onset"]
    def fnum(v, spec):
        return "n/a" if v is None else format(v, spec)

    report.append(f"  onset: измерено {fnum(on['measured_onset_s'], '.3f')} s (ожидалось "
                  f"{on['expected_onset_s']:.3f} s, отклонение {fnum(on['deviation_s'], '+.3f')} s); "
                  f"SNR L {fnum(on['confidence']['snr_db_L'], '.0f')} / "
                  f"R {fnum(on['confidence']['snr_db_R'], '.0f')} dB; "
                  f"residual L {fnum(on['confidence']['residual_fraction_L'], '.3f')}; "
                  f"reliable={on['reliable']}; сдвиг применён={on['applied']}")
    if on["applied"]:
        report.append(f"  onset: диапазоны состояний сдвинуты на {on['offset_s']:+.3f} с")
    if validity_reasons:
        report.append("  ПРИЧИНЫ НЕВАЛИДНОСТИ: " + "; ".join(validity_reasons))
    report.append("IDLE BASELINE STABILITY (pre/post, dBFS; drift >3 dB is warning):")
    for ch in ("L", "R"):
        s = summaries[ch]
        post_text = f"{s['baseline_post_dbfs']:.2f}" if s["baseline_post_dbfs"] is not None else "n/a"
        drift_text = f"{s['baseline_drift_db']:+.2f} dB" if s["baseline_drift_db"] is not None else "n/a"
        stable_text = "stable" if s["baseline_stable"] is True else ("DRIFT" if s["baseline_stable"] is False else "not checked")
        report.append(f"  ADC {ch}: pre={s['baseline_pre_dbfs']:.2f}; post={post_text}; drift={drift_text} [{stable_text}]")
    report.append("IDLE ULTRASONIC BASELINE (integrated dBFS; ASD median dBFS/√Hz; pre → post):")
    for ch in ("L", "R"):
        for label, band in summaries[ch]["idle_ultrasonic_bands"].items():
            pre = band.get("idle_pre")
            post = band.get("idle_post")
            if pre is None and post is None:
                continue
            ptxt = f"{pre['dbfs_median']:.2f}/{pre['asd_median_dbfs_sqrt_hz']:.2f}" if pre else "n/a"
            qtxt = f"{post['dbfs_median']:.2f}/{post['asd_median_dbfs_sqrt_hz']:.2f}" if post else "n/a"
            report.append(f"  ADC {ch} {label}: {ptxt} → {qtxt}")
    report.append("ACTIVE CARRIER CONTINUITY (short-window tone fits; dropout = ≥20 ms at least 20 dB below measured reference):")
    for state in ("L_steady", "R_steady"):
        tc = tone_continuity[state]
        if not tc.get("available"):
            report.append(f"  {state}: unavailable — {tc.get('reason', 'unknown reason')}")
            continue
        episode_text = ", ".join(
            f"#{e['id']} {e['start_s']:.3f}–{e['end_s']:.3f}s ({e['duration_ms']:.0f}ms; min {e['min_tone_dbfs']:.1f} dBFS)"
            for e in tc["episodes"]
        ) or "нет"
        report.append(f"  {state} ADC {tc['channel']}: subwindow={tc['subwindow_ms']:.1f} ms; "
                      f"ref={tc['reference_dbfs']:.2f} dBFS; median={tc['median_tone_dbfs']:.2f}; "
                      f"p01={tc['p01_tone_dbfs']:.2f}; "
                      f"min={tc['min_tone_dbfs']:.2f}; below-threshold={tc['below_threshold_subwindows']}/"
                      f"{tc['subwindows']}; dropouts={tc['dropout_episodes']}; "
                      f"total={tc['total_dropout_ms']:.0f} ms; longest={tc['longest_dropout_ms']:.0f} ms")
        if tc["episodes"]:
            report.append("    events: " + episode_text)
    report.append("STEADY RESIDUAL EXCURSIONS: median / p95 / max dBFS; adjacent windows grouped into episodes; threshold=idle +6 dB")
    report.append("STATE       ADC    20–20k median / p95 / max              windows  episodes  longest-event  40k–Nyquist median / p95 / max")
    for ch in ("L", "R"):
        st = summaries[ch]
        report.append(f"IDLE BASELINE ADC {ch}: {st['baseline_dbfs']:.2f} dBFS")
        for state in ("L_steady", "R_steady"):
            q = st["states"].get(state)
            if not q:
                continue
            a = q["residual_20_20k"]; b = q["raw_40_nyquist"]
            high_band = (f"{b['median']:8.2f} / {b['p95']:8.2f} / {b['max']:8.2f}"
                         if b is not None else "       n/a /      n/a /      n/a")
            report.append(f"{state:10s} {ch:3s}  {a['median']:8.2f} / {a['p95']:8.2f} / {a['max']:8.2f}  "
                          f"{a['bursts']:4d}/{q['windows']:<4d}  {a['episodes']:4d}  {a['longest_episode_s']:6.2f} s  {high_band}")
            for label, band in q["raw_ultrasonic_bands"].items():
                if band is None:
                    continue
                report.append(f"    {label:7s}: level median/p95/max={band['dbfs_median']:.2f}/{band['dbfs_p95']:.2f}/{band['dbfs_max']:.2f} dBFS; "
                              f"ASD median/p95={band['asd_median_dbfs_sqrt_hz']:.2f}/{band['asd_p95_dbfs_sqrt_hz']:.2f} dBFS/√Hz")
    report.append("SIMULTANEOUS L/R EXCURSIONS (same windows, each channel's idle+6 dB threshold):")
    for state, st in shared_stats.items():
        corr = f"{st['correlation_db']:+.3f}" if st["correlation_db"] is not None else "n/a"
        report.append(f"  {state}: corr(residual dB)={corr}; L={st.get('L_excursion_windows', 0)}, "
                      f"R={st.get('R_excursion_windows', 0)}, simultaneous={st['both_excursion_windows']}, "
                      f"shared episodes={st['shared_episodes']} of {st['windows']} windows")
    report.append("TOP WINDOWS BY 20–20k RESIDUAL:")
    for ch in ("L", "R"):
        base = summaries[ch]["baseline_dbfs"]
        rows = [r for r in all_rows if r["channel"] == ch and r["state"] in ("L_steady", "R_steady")]
        rows.sort(key=lambda r: r["residual_20_20k_dbfs"], reverse=True)
        for r in rows[:8]:
            report.append(f"  ADC {ch} t={r['time_s']:.2f}s {r['state']:<9s} {r['residual_20_20k_dbfs']:.2f} dBFS "
                          f"({r['residual_20_20k_dbfs'] - base:+.2f} dB vs idle)")
    report.append("NOTES:")
    report.append("- Fundamental level is measured in the actual ADC recording; it is not inferred from the ALSA mixer percentage.")
    report.append("- Harmonics H1..Hn are removed by least-squares projection separately for each analysis window.")
    report.append("- 40k–Nyquist is a raw band level; no harmonic subtraction is applied there (H1..H20 are below 20 kHz at 997 Hz).")
    report.append("- Residual excursions and carrier dropouts are separate measurements: a missing carrier is not classified as additive noise.")
    report.append("- Carrier continuity is estimated on short contiguous windows; threshold events require the minimum consecutive duration. Times use the capture-recording time axis.")
    report.append("- Residual excursion windows are not independent physical events; adjacent windows are grouped into episodes.")
    report.append("- Idle baseline uses both long pre-test and post-test silence; their difference is reported separately.")
    report.append("- Ultrasonic bands report integrated dBFS and median/p95 spectral density in dBFS/√Hz.")
    report.append("- tone-continuity.csv contains the short-window active-carrier track; it is separate from the broadband residual timeline.")
    report.append("- This test localizes DAC/ADC paths; it does not identify a PCB component.")
    report.append("- Cross-channel tone levels are coherent projections at the measured source frequency, not broadband residual levels.")
    summary = "\n".join(report) + "\n"
    (outdir / "summary.txt").write_text(summary, encoding="utf-8")
    machine_report = {
        "schema": 1, "created": stamp, "playback_device": play_dev, "capture_device": rec_dev,
        "playback_label": _device_label(play_dev), "capture_label": _device_label(rec_dev),
        "playback_format": play.fmt, "capture_format": capture.fmt, "sample_rate": fs,
        "tone_hz_nominal": tone_hz, "tone_level_dbfs": tone_level_dbfs,
        "target_adc_dbfs": target_dbfs, "tolerance_db": tolerance_db,
        "calibration_trials": calibration_history, "series_level_valid": series_valid,
        "duration_per_channel_s": duration_s, "window_s": window_s, "hop_s": hop_s,
        "idle_seconds_each_end": idle_s, "gap_seconds": gap_s,
        "max_harmonics_removed": max_harmonics, "transition_guard_s": transition_guard_s,
        "measured_tones": tone_levels, "channel_map": tone_map,
        "io_health": io_health, "validity_reasons": validity_reasons,
        "tone_continuity": tone_continuity, "tone_continuity_csv": str(tone_csv_file),
        "analysis": summaries, "simultaneous_excursions": shared_stats,
    }
    (outdir / "steady.json").write_text(json.dumps(machine_report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print_fn(summary)
    print_fn(f"Результаты сохранены: {outdir}")
    print_fn("Нужны: summary.txt, steady.json, timeline.csv, tone-continuity.csv и при событиях capture-float32.wav")
    return outdir


================================================================================
FILE: valvebench/store.py

"""Сохранение результатов: JSON (полностью) и CSV (кривые) для сравнения «до/после»."""
from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import hashlib
import json
import math
import os
import re
import shutil
import tempfile
import time
from datetime import datetime

from .measure import to_jsonable

DIR = os.path.expanduser("~/.local/share/valvebench")


def save_json(res, path=None, label=""):
    os.makedirs(DIR, exist_ok=True)
    path = path or os.path.join(DIR, time.strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S-") + res.get("kind", "res")
                                + (f"-{label}" if label else "") + ".json")
    d = to_jsonable(res)
    d["saved"] = time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
    d["label"] = label
    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(d, f, ensure_ascii=False)
    return path


def load_json(path):
    with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return json.load(f)


def sweep_csv(res, path):
    """Кривые свипа в CSV: freq, отклонение/АЧХ и THD по каналам."""
    f = res["ch"]["L"]["freq"]
    tf = res["ch"]["L"]["thd_freq"]
    with open(path, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        w = csv.writer(fh)
        w.writerow(["freq_hz", "fr_norm_L_db", "fr_norm_R_db", "riaa_dev_L_db", "riaa_dev_R_db"])
        for i in range(len(f)):
            w.writerow([f"{f[i]:.3f}", f"{res['ch']['L']['fr_norm_db'][i]:.4f}",
                        f"{res['ch']['R']['fr_norm_db'][i]:.4f}",
                        f"{res['ch']['L']['riaa_dev_db'][i]:.4f}", f"{res['ch']['R']['riaa_dev_db'][i]:.4f}"])
        w.writerow([])
        w.writerow(["freq_hz", "thd_L_pct", "thd_R_pct"])
        for i in range(len(tf)):
            w.writerow([f"{tf[i]:.3f}", f"{res['ch']['L']['thd_pct'][i]:.6f}", f"{res['ch']['R']['thd_pct'][i]:.6f}"])



def save_gain_series(res, directory=None):
    """Save a Gain Match frequency series under the current project directory.

    Unlike the generic legacy save_json() default, this deliberately avoids
    the home directory: default output is ./measurements/gain-match/.
    Returns (json_path, csv_path).
    """
    directory = directory or os.path.join(os.getcwd(), "measurements", "gain-match")
    os.makedirs(directory, exist_ok=True)
    stamp = time.strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S")
    stem = f"gain-match-{stamp}"
    suffix = 1
    while os.path.exists(os.path.join(directory, stem + ".json")):
        stem = f"gain-match-{stamp}-{suffix:02d}"
        suffix += 1
    json_path = os.path.join(directory, stem + ".json")
    csv_path = os.path.join(directory, stem + ".csv")

    payload = to_jsonable(res)
    payload["saved"] = time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
    tmp_json = json_path + ".tmp"
    with open(tmp_json, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        json.dump(payload, fh, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        fh.write("\n")
    os.replace(tmp_json, json_path)

    with open(csv_path, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        writer = csv.writer(fh)
        writer.writerow([
            "frequency_hz", "delta_adc_L_minus_R_db", "loopback_correction_db",
            "corrected_delta_gain_L_minus_R_db", "repeat_spread_db", "SINAD_L_db",
            "SINAD_R_db", "carrier_residual_L_db", "carrier_residual_R_db",
            "valid", "repeat_stable", "repeatability",
        ])
        for row in res.get("rows", []):
            left, right = row["channels"]["L"], row["channels"]["R"]
            writer.writerow([
                f"{row['frequency_hz']:.6f}", f"{row['delta_adc_db']:.6f}",
                "" if row["loopback_correction_db"] is None else f"{row['loopback_correction_db']:.6f}",
                "" if row["delta_gain_db"] is None else f"{row['delta_gain_db']:.6f}",
                f"{row['delta_output_spread_db']:.6f}", f"{left['sinad_db']:.2f}",
                f"{right['sinad_db']:.2f}", f"{left['carrier_residual_db']:.2f}",
                f"{right['carrier_residual_db']:.2f}", int(row["valid"]),
                ("" if row.get("repeat_stable") is None else int(row["repeat_stable"])),
                res.get("repeatability", "assessed"),
            ])
    return json_path, csv_path


def save_gain_reference(reference, directory=None):
    """Persist the per-device loopback correction in the project directory."""
    directory = directory or os.path.join(os.getcwd(), "measurements", "gain-match")
    os.makedirs(directory, exist_ok=True)
    import re
    slug = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9_.-]+", "_", str(reference.get("device_key", "device"))).strip("_.") or "device"
    level_tag = f"{float(reference['generator_level_dbfs']):.1f}".replace("-", "m").replace(".", "p")
    path = os.path.join(directory,
                        f"loopback-reference-{slug}-{int(reference['fs'])}Hz-{level_tag}dBFS.json")
    tmp = path + ".tmp"
    with open(tmp, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        json.dump(to_jsonable(reference), fh, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        fh.write("\n")
    os.replace(tmp, path)
    return path


def load_gain_reference(device_key, fs, level_dbfs, frequencies, directory=None):
    """Load only a reference whose device, sample rate, level and tones all match."""
    directory = directory or os.path.join(os.getcwd(), "measurements", "gain-match")
    import glob
    paths = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(directory, "loopback-reference-*.json")),
                   key=os.path.getmtime, reverse=True)
    wanted = {round(float(f), 6) for f in frequencies}
    for path in paths:
        try:
            with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                ref = json.load(fh)
            if ref.get("kind") != "gain_match_loopback_reference":
                continue
            if ref.get("device_key") != device_key or int(ref.get("fs", -1)) != int(fs):
                continue
            if abs(float(ref.get("generator_level_dbfs", 999.0)) - float(level_dbfs)) > 1e-6:
                continue
            got = {round(float(f), 6) for f in ref.get("frequencies_hz", [])}
            if got != wanted or not all(row.get("valid") for row in ref.get("rows", [])):
                continue
            return ref
        except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError, KeyError):
            continue
    return None


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- device measurement sessions

_DEVICE_ID_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,63}$")
_CURVE_AXES = ("freq", "frequency_hz", "frequency", "freq_hz", "thd_freq",
               "centers", "levels", "levels_dbfs", "gen", "x")


def _clean_json(value):
    """Convert a nested payload to strict JSON (NaN/Inf become null)."""
    value = to_jsonable(value)
    def clean(item):
        if isinstance(item, dict):
            return {str(k): clean(v) for k, v in item.items()}
        if isinstance(item, list):
            return [clean(v) for v in item]
        if isinstance(item, float) and not math.isfinite(item):
            return None
        return item
    return clean(value)


def _is_numeric_vector(value, allow_missing=False):
    if not isinstance(value, list) or not value:
        return False
    return all((allow_missing and v is None) or
               (isinstance(v, (int, float)) and not isinstance(v, bool) and math.isfinite(float(v)))
               for v in value)


def _write_metrics_csv(payload, path):
    """Flat index of scalar metadata/metrics; full structured data stays in JSON."""
    rows = []
    def walk(value, prefix):
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            for key in sorted(value):
                walk(value[key], f"{prefix}.{key}" if prefix else str(key))
        elif isinstance(value, list):
            # Arrays are represented in curves.csv / session.json, not flattened
            # into thousands of hard-to-read metric rows.
            return
        elif value is None or isinstance(value, (str, int, float, bool)):
            rows.append((prefix, value))
    walk(payload, "")
    with open(path, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        writer = csv.writer(fh)
        writer.writerow(("metric_path", "value"))
        for key, value in rows:
            writer.writerow((key, "" if value is None else value))


def _write_curves_csv(result, path):
    """Export numeric x/y vectors as long-form CSV without discarding source arrays."""
    written = 0
    with open(path, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        writer = csv.writer(fh)
        writer.writerow(("group", "x_name", "x_value", "series", "y_value"))

        def walk(value, prefix):
            nonlocal written
            if not isinstance(value, dict):
                return
            axes = [(key, value[key]) for key in _CURVE_AXES
                    if key in value and _is_numeric_vector(value[key])]
            for axis_name, x_values in axes:
                for series_name, y_values in value.items():
                    if series_name == axis_name or not _is_numeric_vector(y_values, allow_missing=True):
                        continue
                    if len(y_values) != len(x_values):
                        continue
                    for x, y in zip(x_values, y_values):
                        writer.writerow((prefix, axis_name, x, series_name, "" if y is None else y))
                        written += 1
            for key, child in value.items():
                if isinstance(child, dict):
                    walk(child, f"{prefix}.{key}" if prefix else str(key))
        walk(result, "result")
    return written


def device_measurements_root():
    """Return the measurements folder beside the active ValveBench project.

    Resolve from this module rather than the process working directory: a
    desktop launcher, shell shortcut, or ``python -m`` invocation may start
    with an unrelated cwd (including the home directory).
    """
    project_root = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.realpath(__file__)))
    return os.path.join(project_root, "measurements", "devices")


def save_device_measurement(result, device_id, note="", context=None, directory=None):
    """Save one DUT measurement as a portable, immutable session directory.

    Default location is <ValveBench-project>/measurements/devices/<device-id>/.
    The original numeric arrays are retained in session.json; CSV files are
    convenience indexes for spreadsheets and quick review. ``directory`` is
    primarily intended for tests and explicit programmatic exports.
    Returns the final session directory.
    """
    device_id = str(device_id or "").strip()
    if not _DEVICE_ID_RE.fullmatch(device_id):
        raise ValueError("ID аппарата: 1–64 символа; допустимы латинские буквы, цифры, точка, дефис и подчёркивание; первый символ — буква или цифра")
    if not isinstance(result, dict) or not result:
        raise ValueError("нет результата измерения для сохранения")

    created = datetime.now().astimezone().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
    payload_result = _clean_json(result)
    inherited_context = payload_result.pop("_valvebench_context", None)
    measurement_context = _clean_json(context if context else inherited_context or {})
    kind = str(payload_result.get("kind") or "measurement")
    safe_kind = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9_-]+", "-", kind).strip("-")[:32] or "measurement"
    root = os.path.abspath(directory or device_measurements_root())
    os.makedirs(root, exist_ok=True)
    root_real = os.path.realpath(root)
    device_root = os.path.join(root_real, device_id)
    if os.path.islink(device_root):
        raise ValueError("каталог аппарата является символической ссылкой; экспорт остановлен")
    if os.path.commonpath((root_real, os.path.realpath(device_root))) != root_real:
        raise ValueError("путь каталога аппарата выходит за пределы каталога измерений")
    os.makedirs(device_root, exist_ok=True)

    stamp = datetime.now().astimezone().strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S")
    stem = f"{stamp}-{safe_kind}"
    final = os.path.join(device_root, stem)
    suffix = 1
    while os.path.exists(final):
        final = os.path.join(device_root, f"{stem}-{suffix:02d}")
        suffix += 1

    envelope = {
        "format": "valvebench-device-measurement",
        "schema": 1,
        "device_id": device_id,
        "session_id": os.path.basename(final),
        "created_at_local": created,
        "note": str(note or "").strip(),
        "result_kind": kind,
        "measurement_context": measurement_context,
        "result": payload_result,
    }

    staging = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=".pending-", dir=device_root)
    try:
        json_path = os.path.join(staging, "session.json")
        with open(json_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            json.dump(envelope, fh, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, allow_nan=False)
            fh.write("\n")
            fh.flush()
            os.fsync(fh.fileno())
        metrics_path = os.path.join(staging, "metrics.csv")
        _write_metrics_csv(envelope, metrics_path)
        curves_path = os.path.join(staging, "curves.csv")
        curve_rows = _write_curves_csv(payload_result, curves_path)
        readme_path = os.path.join(staging, "README.txt")
        with open(readme_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            fh.write("ValveBench device measurement session\n")
            fh.write(f"Device ID: {device_id}\nSession: {os.path.basename(final)}\n")
            fh.write(f"Created: {created}\nType: {kind}\n")
            if note:
                fh.write(f"Note: {str(note).strip()}\n")
            fh.write("\nFiles:\n  session.json  complete result and measurement context\n")
            fh.write("  metrics.csv   scalar values and metadata\n")
            fh.write(f"  curves.csv    numeric curves ({curve_rows} rows)\n")
            fh.write("\nThe JSON file is authoritative; CSV files are convenience exports.\n")
            fh.write("Raw PCM samples are not added by this generic exporter unless the measurement result itself contains them.\n")
        manifest = {}
        for name in ("session.json", "metrics.csv", "curves.csv", "README.txt"):
            file_path = os.path.join(staging, name)
            digest = hashlib.sha256()
            with open(file_path, "rb") as fh:
                for block in iter(lambda: fh.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
                    digest.update(block)
            manifest[name] = {"size_bytes": os.path.getsize(file_path), "sha256": digest.hexdigest()}
        with open(os.path.join(staging, "MANIFEST.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            json.dump({"schema": 1, "files": manifest}, fh, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
            fh.write("\n")
            fh.flush()
            os.fsync(fh.fileno())
        os.replace(staging, final)
    except Exception:
        shutil.rmtree(staging, ignore_errors=True)
        raise
    return final

FILE: valvebench/ui.py

"""GTK4 / libadwaita интерфейс. Вся логика измерений — в measure.py; здесь только формы,
графики и фоновые потоки (звук никогда не трогается из потока GUI)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import sys
import threading
import math
import os
import time
import hashlib

import numpy as np
import gi

gi.require_version("Gtk", "4.0")
gi.require_version("Adw", "1")
from gi.repository import Adw, GLib, Gtk  # noqa: E402

from . import __version__, calib, dsp, measure as m, signals, store, vinyl, preferences  # noqa: E402
from .identity import application_id as _resolved_application_id  # noqa: E402
from . import gainmatch  # noqa: E402
from .gainmatch import GainTracker  # noqa: E402
from .gainview import GainDeltaView  # noqa: E402

                      stable_device_identity)  # noqa: E402
from .plot import Plot  # noqa: E402
from . import livemetrics as lm  # noqa: E402

from .analysis import AnalysisWorker, spectrum_job  # noqa: E402
from .widgets import (MeterStrip, format_analysis_rows, draw_analysis_card,  # noqa: E402
                      CH_COLORS)

RATES = [44100, 48000, 88200, 96000, 176400, 192000]


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------- виджеты


def _pref_widget_name(title):
    return "pref_" + hashlib.sha1(str(title).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:12]


def _set_label_text_if_changed(label, value):
    value = str(value)
    if label.get_text() != value:
        label.set_text(value)


def spin(title, lo, hi, step, val, digits=1, subtitle=None):
    r = Adw.SpinRow.new_with_range(lo, hi, step)
    r.set_name(_pref_widget_name(title))
    r.set_title(title)
    r.set_digits(digits)
    r.set_value(val)
    if subtitle:
        r.set_subtitle(subtitle)
    return r


def combo(title, items, sel=0, subtitle=None):
    r = Adw.ComboRow(title=title)
    r.set_name(_pref_widget_name(title))
    r.set_model(Gtk.StringList.new(list(items)))
    r.set_selected(sel)
    if subtitle:
        r.set_subtitle(subtitle)
    return r


def group(title, *rows):
    g = Adw.PreferencesGroup(title=title)
    for r in rows:
        g.add(r)
    return g


def button(label, cb, primary=False):
    b = Gtk.Button(label=label)
    if primary:
        b.add_css_class("suggested-action")
        b.add_css_class("pill")
    b.connect("clicked", lambda *_: cb())
    return b


def _spectrum_ylim(traces, top=0.0):
    """Choose a readable spectrum floor from the actual curve and card noise floor."""
    arrays = []
    for trace in traces:
        values = np.asarray(trace.get("y", []), dtype=float)
        values = values[np.isfinite(values)]
        if values.size:
            arrays.append(values)
    if not arrays:
        return -160.0, float(top)
    values = np.concatenate(arrays)
    low = float(np.nanpercentile(values, 5.0)) - 10.0
    # Avoid both a useless fixed −140 dB limit and an absurdly tall plot
    # when a single isolated bin sits near numerical zero.
    low = min(-80.0, max(-240.0, low))
    return low, float(top)


def _noise_floor_trace(profile, channel, fs, nfft, average=1.0, line_bins=True):
    """Convert a saved per-card ADC noise-density profile to the graph's units.

    The same measured density is shared by Spectrum/THD/IMD. Line-bin views add
    the analysis-window ENBW; an averaged time waveform (IMD) reduces random
    noise power by the number of independent blocks.
    """
    if not isinstance(profile, dict):
        return None
    pf = np.asarray(profile.get("frequency_hz", []), dtype=float)
    pd = np.asarray(profile.get("channels", {}).get(channel, {}).get("density_dbfs_sqrt_hz", []), dtype=float)
    if pf.size < 2 or pd.size != pf.size:
        return None
    y = pd.copy()
    if line_bins:
        w = dsp.bh(int(nfft))
        enbw = float(fs) * float(np.sum(w * w)) / max(float(np.sum(w) ** 2), 1e-30)
        enbw = enbw / max(1.0, float(average))
        y += 10.0 * np.log10(max(enbw, 1e-30))
    return {"x": pf, "y": y, "label": f"Шум АЦП {channel}",
            "color": "ref", "dash": True, "width": 1.2}


def textview():
    tv = Gtk.TextView(editable=False, cursor_visible=False, monospace=True,
                      left_margin=10, top_margin=8, bottom_margin=8)
    sw = Gtk.ScrolledWindow(min_content_height=170)
    sw.set_child(tv)
    sw.set_policy(Gtk.PolicyType.AUTOMATIC, Gtk.PolicyType.AUTOMATIC)
    return tv.get_buffer(), sw


def vertical_page_scroller(child):
    """Make the complete contents of a page vertically reachable on short screens.

    Keep the vertical scrollbar non-overlayed so it remains visible when the
    page is taller than the window. Horizontal scrolling is intentionally off:
    controls should wrap/reflow rather than create a second navigation axis.
    """
    scroller = Gtk.ScrolledWindow()
    scroller.set_policy(Gtk.PolicyType.NEVER, Gtk.PolicyType.AUTOMATIC)
    scroller.set_overlay_scrolling(False)
    scroller.set_kinetic_scrolling(True)
    scroller.set_hexpand(True)
    scroller.set_vexpand(True)
    scroller.set_child(child)
    return scroller


class Page(Gtk.Box):
    """Общая измерительная страница с постоянным статусом и управлением масштабом."""

    def __init__(self, win, *param_widgets, plot=None):
        # 0.1.20: настройки слева прокручиваются отдельно и изменяют ширину (Paned), а правая
        # часть — график — занимает всё остальное место, а не прячется под длинной колонкой
        # настроек. Страница остаётся дочерним элементом Stack (маршрутизация статуса прежняя).
        super().__init__(orientation=Gtk.Orientation.VERTICAL)
        self.win = win
        self._job_button_states = None
        left = Gtk.Box(orientation=Gtk.Orientation.VERTICAL, spacing=12,
                       margin_top=12, margin_bottom=12, margin_start=12, margin_end=6)
        for w in param_widgets:
            left.append(w)
        self.page_scroller = vertical_page_scroller(left)
        self.page_scroller.set_min_content_width(300)

        right = Gtk.Box(orientation=Gtk.Orientation.VERTICAL, spacing=8,
                        margin_top=12, margin_bottom=12, margin_start=6, margin_end=12)
        right.set_hexpand(True)
        status_bar = Gtk.Box(orientation=Gtk.Orientation.HORIZONTAL, spacing=8)
        status_bar.add_css_class("card")
        status_bar.set_margin_bottom(3)
        self.job_spinner = Gtk.Spinner()
        self.job_status = Gtk.Label(
            label="ГОТОВО К ИЗМЕРЕНИЮ · выбери параметры и нажми кнопку слева",
            xalign=0, wrap=False)
        self.job_status.add_css_class("title-4")
        self.job_status.set_hexpand(True)
        self.job_status.set_single_line_mode(True)
        self.job_status.set_ellipsize(3)  # Pango.EllipsizeMode.END
        self.job_status.set_max_width_chars(84)
        status_bar.append(self.job_spinner)
        status_bar.append(self.job_status)
        right.append(status_bar)

        self.plot = plot
        if plot is not None:
            plot.set_content_height(380)
            right.append(plot)
            scale_bar = Gtk.Box(orientation=Gtk.Orientation.HORIZONTAL, spacing=7)
            scale_bar.set_margin_top(2)
            scale_bar.set_margin_bottom(4)
            scale_bar.append(Gtk.Label(label="Масштаб Y:", xalign=0))
            scale_bar.append(Gtk.Label(label="от", xalign=0))
            self.y_min = Gtk.SpinButton.new_with_range(-300.0, 100.0, 0.1)
            self.y_min.set_name("plot_y_min")
            self.y_min.set_digits(2)
            self.y_min.set_size_request(86, -1)
            self.y_min.set_value(float(plot.ylim[0]))
            scale_bar.append(self.y_min)
            scale_bar.append(Gtk.Label(label="до", xalign=0))
            self.y_max = Gtk.SpinButton.new_with_range(-300.0, 100.0, 0.1)
            self.y_max.set_name("plot_y_max")
            self.y_max.set_digits(2)
            self.y_max.set_size_request(86, -1)
            self.y_max.set_value(float(plot.ylim[1]))
            scale_bar.append(self.y_max)
            scale_bar.append(button("Применить", self.apply_plot_scale))
            scale_bar.append(button("Авто", self.auto_plot_scale))
            self.plot_scale_hint = Gtk.Label(label="", xalign=0)
            self.plot_scale_hint.add_css_class("dim-label")
            scale_bar.append(self.plot_scale_hint)
            scale_bar.set_hexpand(True)
            right.append(scale_bar)
            plot.set_view_changed_callback(self._sync_plot_scale)
        else:
            self.y_min = self.y_max = self.plot_scale_hint = None

        self.buf, sw = textview()
        sw.set_min_content_height(150)
        right.append(sw)
        self.right_scroller = vertical_page_scroller(right)

        self.paned = Gtk.Paned(orientation=Gtk.Orientation.HORIZONTAL)
        self.paned.set_wide_handle(True)
        self.paned.set_start_child(self.page_scroller)
        self.paned.set_end_child(self.right_scroller)
        self.paned.set_resize_start_child(False)
        self.paned.set_shrink_start_child(False)
        self.paned.set_resize_end_child(True)
        self.paned.set_shrink_end_child(False)
        self.paned.set_position(340)
        self.paned.set_hexpand(True)
        self.paned.set_vexpand(True)
        self.append(self.paned)

    def _sync_plot_scale(self, ylim):
        if self.y_min is None:
            return
        self.y_min.set_value(float(ylim[0]))
        self.y_max.set_value(float(ylim[1]))
        if self.plot.manual_ylim is None:
            self.plot_scale_hint.set_text("авто")
        else:
            self.plot_scale_hint.set_text("ручной масштаб")

    def apply_plot_scale(self):
        if self.plot is None:
            return
        lo, hi = self.y_min.get_value(), self.y_max.get_value()
        try:
            self.plot.set_manual_ylim(lo, hi)
            self.plot_scale_hint.set_text(f"Ручной диапазон {lo:g}…{hi:g}")
            self.win.persist_plot_scale(self)
        except ValueError as exc:
            self.plot_scale_hint.set_text(str(exc))

    def auto_plot_scale(self):
        if self.plot is None:
            return
        self.plot.clear_manual_ylim()
        self.plot_scale_hint.set_text("Автомасштаб")
        self.win.clear_plot_scale_preference(self)

    @staticmethod
    def _walk_widgets(root):
        child = root.get_first_child()
        while child is not None:
            yield child
            yield from Page._walk_widgets(child)
            child = child.get_next_sibling()

    def set_job_busy(self, busy):
        if busy:
            if self._job_button_states is None:
                self._job_button_states = [(w, w.get_sensitive()) for w in self._walk_widgets(self)
                                           if isinstance(w, Gtk.Button)]
                for widget, _state in self._job_button_states:
                    widget.set_sensitive(False)
        elif self._job_button_states is not None:
            for widget, was_sensitive in self._job_button_states:
                widget.set_sensitive(was_sensitive)
            self._job_button_states = None

    def set_job_status(self, state, message, elapsed=None):
        if state == "running":
            self.job_spinner.start()
            text = f"ИЗМЕРЕНИЕ ВЫПОЛНЯЕТСЯ · {message}"
        else:
            self.job_spinner.stop()
            if state == "success":
                suffix = f" · {elapsed:.1f} с" if elapsed is not None else ""
                text = f"ИЗМЕРЕНИЕ ЗАВЕРШЕНО{suffix} · результат ниже"
            elif state == "error":
                text = f"ИЗМЕРЕНИЕ НЕ ВЫПОЛНЕНО · {message}"
            else:
                text = str(message)
        self.job_status.set_text(text)
        self.job_status.set_tooltip_text(text)

    def say(self, txt):
        self.buf.set_text(txt)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------------ страницы


class GeneratorPage(Page):
    """Расширенная панель генератора и простой осциллограф буфера/входа АЦП."""

    WAVEFORMS = ["Синус", "Треугольник", "Меандр", "Пила", "Белый шум", "Розовый шум"]
    WAVEFORM_KEYS = ["sine", "triangle", "square", "sawtooth", "white", "pink"]

    def __init__(self, win):
        self.win = win
        self.waveform = combo("Форма сигнала", self.WAVEFORMS,
                              self.WAVEFORM_KEYS.index(getattr(win, "generator_waveform", "sine")))
        self.frequency = spin("Частота, Гц", 5, 40000, 1, win.live.f.get_value(), 0,
                              "Быстрая настройка также доступна на странице Gain Match")
        self.level = spin("Уровень, dBFS", -100, -0.5, 0.5, win.live.lv.get_value(), 1)
        self.route = combo("Выход", ["L + R", "Только L", "Только R"], win.live.route.get_selected())
        self.noise_lo = spin("Шум: нижняя частота, Гц", 1, 40000, 1,
                             getattr(win, "generator_noise_low_hz", 20.0), 0)
        self.noise_hi = spin("Шум: верхняя частота, Гц", 100, 86000, 10,
                             getattr(win, "generator_noise_high_hz", 20000.0), 0)
        self.scope_source = combo("Что показывать", ["Буфер генератора · до ЦАП", "Вход АЦП · реальный сигнал"], 0)
        self.window_ms = spin("Окно осциллографа, мс", 1, 100, 1, 10, 0)
        self.scope_status = Gtk.Label(label="Расчётный сигнал до ЦАП", xalign=0, wrap=True)
        self.scope_status.add_css_class("dim-label")
        self._syncing = False
        self.timer = None
        self._last_preview_signature = None
        self.plot_widget = Plot(False, "Время, мс", "Амплитуда, FS")
        self.plot_widget.set_view((0, 10), (-1.1, 1.1))
        scope_group = group("Осциллограф", self.scope_source, self.window_ms)
        super().__init__(win,
                         group("Быстрые параметры генератора", self.frequency, self.level, self.route),
                         group("Форма и спектр", self.waveform, self.noise_lo, self.noise_hi),
                         scope_group, self.scope_status,
                         plot=self.plot_widget)
        # Два набора быстрых регуляторов остаются синхронными в обе стороны.
        for a, b in ((self.frequency, win.live.f), (self.level, win.live.lv)):
            a.connect("notify::value", lambda *_args, src=a, dst=b: self._sync_value(src, dst))
            b.connect("notify::value", lambda *_args, src=b, dst=a: self._sync_value(src, dst))
        self.route.connect("notify::selected", lambda *_: self._sync_route(self.route, win.live.route))
        win.live.route.connect("notify::selected", lambda *_: self._sync_route(win.live.route, self.route))
        self.waveform.connect("notify::selected", self._waveform_changed)
        self.noise_lo.connect("notify::value", self._noise_changed)
        self.noise_hi.connect("notify::value", self._noise_changed)
        self.scope_source.connect("notify::selected", lambda *_: self._scope_source_changed())
        self.window_ms.connect("notify::value", lambda *_: self.update_scope())
        self._apply_waveform_controls()

    def _scope_source_changed(self):
        self._last_preview_signature = None
        self.update_scope()

    def _sync_value(self, src, dst):
        if self._syncing:
            return
        self._syncing = True
        try:
            dst.set_value(src.get_value())
        finally:
            self._syncing = False
        self.update_scope()

    def _sync_route(self, src, dst):
        if self._syncing:
            return
        self._syncing = True
        try:
            dst.set_selected(src.get_selected())
        finally:
            self._syncing = False
        self.update_scope()

    def _waveform_changed(self, *_):
        self.win.generator_waveform = self.WAVEFORM_KEYS[self.waveform.get_selected()]
        self._apply_waveform_controls()
        if self.win.generator_btn.get_active():
            self.win.live.apply()
        self.update_scope()

    def _noise_changed(self, *_):
        self.win.generator_noise_low_hz = float(self.noise_lo.get_value())
        self.win.generator_noise_high_hz = float(self.noise_hi.get_value())
        self.update_scope()
        if self.win.generator_btn.get_active():
            self.win.live.apply()

    def _apply_waveform_controls(self):
        noise = self.WAVEFORM_KEYS[self.waveform.get_selected()] in ("white", "pink")
        self.noise_lo.set_sensitive(noise)
        self.noise_hi.set_sensitive(noise)

    def set_visible(self, visible):
        if visible and self.timer is None:
            self.update_scope()
            self.timer = GLib.timeout_add(200, self._scope_tick)
        elif not visible and self.timer is not None:
            GLib.source_remove(self.timer)
            self.timer = None

    def _scope_tick(self):
        if self.win.stack.get_visible_child() is not self:
            self.timer = None
            return False
        self.update_scope()
        return True

    def update_scope(self):
        if not hasattr(self, "plot_widget"):
            return
        fs = int(self.win.rig_fs())
        window_ms = float(self.window_ms.get_value())
        n = max(128, int(fs * window_ms / 1000.0))
        source = self.scope_source.get_selected()
        if source == 1:
            be = self.win.be
            if not self.win.live_on or be is None or not getattr(be, "live_running", False):
                _set_label_text_if_changed(
                    self.scope_status, "Вход АЦП не открыт. Нажми компактную кнопку «АЦП» вверху.")
                if self.plot_widget.traces:
                    self.plot_widget.set_traces([])
                return
            x = np.asarray(be.live_recent(window_ms / 1000.0), dtype=float)
            if x.ndim != 2 or x.shape[0] < 32:
                _set_label_text_if_changed(self.scope_status, "Ожидаю свежие отсчёты АЦП…")
                if self.plot_widget.traces:
                    self.plot_widget.set_traces([])
                return
            if x.shape[0] > 5000:
                step = max(1, x.shape[0] // 5000)
                x = x[::step]
            t_ms = np.arange(len(x), dtype=float) * 1000.0 / fs
            self.plot_widget.set_view((0, max(window_ms, float(t_ms[-1]))), (-1.05, 1.05),
                                      xlabel="Время, мс", ylabel="Вход АЦП, FS")
            self.plot_widget.set_traces([
                {"x": t_ms, "y": x[:, c], "label": ch, "color": ch, "width": 1.3}
                for c, ch in enumerate(("L", "R")) if c < x.shape[1]])
            status = f"Вход АЦП · реальные отсчёты · {fs} Гц · шкала FS"
            _set_label_text_if_changed(self.scope_status, status)
            return

        waveform = self.WAVEFORM_KEYS[self.waveform.get_selected()]
        freq = float(round(self.frequency.get_value()))
        level = float(self.level.get_value())
        route = self.route.get_selected()
        preview_signature = (fs, window_ms, source, waveform, freq, level, route,
                             float(self.noise_lo.get_value()), float(self.noise_hi.get_value()))
        if preview_signature == self._last_preview_signature:
            return
        self._last_preview_signature = preview_signature
        try:
            sig, info = signals.make_generator_signal(
                fs, waveform=waveform, frequency_hz=freq, level_dbfs=level,
                seconds=window_ms / 1000.0,
                noise_low_hz=float(self.noise_lo.get_value()),
                noise_high_hz=float(self.noise_hi.get_value()))
        except ValueError as exc:
            _set_label_text_if_changed(self.scope_status, str(exc))
            if self.plot_widget.traces:
                self.plot_widget.set_traces([])
            return
        sig = np.asarray(sig[:n], dtype=float)
        if sig.size > 5000:
            step = max(1, sig.size // 5000)
            sig = sig[::step]
        t_ms = np.arange(len(sig), dtype=float) * 1000.0 / fs
        traces = []
        if route in (0, 1):
            traces.append({"x": t_ms, "y": sig, "label": "L", "color": "L", "width": 1.3})
        if route in (0, 2):
            traces.append({"x": t_ms, "y": sig, "label": "R", "color": "R", "width": 1.3})
        self.plot_widget.set_view((0, max(window_ms, float(t_ms[-1]) if len(t_ms) else window_ms)),
                                  (-1.05, 1.05), xlabel="Время, мс", ylabel="Буфер ЦАП, FS")
        self.plot_widget.set_traces(traces)
        level_label = (f"RMS {info.get('actual_rms_dbfs', info.get('rms_dbfs', 0.0)):.1f} dBFS"
                       if info.get("level_definition") == "rms_dbfs"
                       else f"Peak {info.get('peak_dbfs', 0.0):.1f} dBFS")
        status = f"Буфер до ЦАП · {waveform} · {level_label} · {fs} Гц"
        _set_label_text_if_changed(self.scope_status, status)


class MeasurementBatchPage(Page):
    """Последовательный DUT-прогон с единым сохраняемым паспортом."""

    def __init__(self, win):
        self.win = win
        self.chk_response = Gtk.CheckButton(label="АЧХ / sweep")
        self.chk_response.set_active(True)
        self.chk_thd = Gtk.CheckButton(label="THD / THD+N")
        self.chk_thd.set_active(True)
        self.chk_noise = Gtk.CheckButton(label="Шум")
        self.chk_noise.set_active(True)
        self.chk_imd = Gtk.CheckButton(label="IMD · 19 + 20 кГц")
        self.chk_imd.set_active(False)
        checks = Gtk.Box(orientation=Gtk.Orientation.VERTICAL, spacing=8)
        for w in (self.chk_response, self.chk_thd, self.chk_noise, self.chk_imd):
            checks.append(w)
        identity_box = Gtk.Box(orientation=Gtk.Orientation.VERTICAL, spacing=6)
        identity_box.append(Gtk.Label(label="Номер проверяемого аппарата", xalign=0))
        identity_box.append(win.measurement_device_id)
        identity_box.append(Gtk.Label(label="Лампы / состояние / примечание", xalign=0))
        identity_box.append(win.measurement_note)
        self.run_button = button("ВЫПОЛНИТЬ ПАКЕТ ИЗМЕРЕНИЙ", self.run_batch, True)
        self.continue_button = button("ПОДКЛЮЧИЛ ANTI-RIAA · ПРОДОЛЖИТЬ", self.run_driven_tests)
        self.continue_button.set_visible(False)
        self.finish_partial_button = button("ЗАВЕРШИТЬ С ТЕКУЩИМИ РЕЗУЛЬТАТАМИ", self.finish_partial)
        self.finish_partial_button.set_visible(False)
        self.save_button = win.export_measurement_btn
        self.save_button.set_label("СОХРАНИТЬ ВЕСЬ ПАКЕТ")
        self.save_button.set_sensitive(False)
        help_text = Gtk.Label(
            label=("Параметры АЧХ, THD, шума и IMD берутся с одноимённых страниц — сначала "
                   "выставь там нужные настройки. Шум измеряется первым: вход DUT должен быть "
                   "нагружен так, как требуется для проверки собственного шума. После шумового "
                   "теста программа остановится и попросит подключить Anti-RIAA. АЧХ, THD и IMD "
                   "выполняются последовательно. Всё сохраняется одной сессией аппарата."),
            xalign=0, wrap=True)
        help_text.add_css_class("dim-label")
        test_title = Gtk.Label(label="Что включить в пакет", xalign=0)
        test_title.add_css_class("heading")
        identity_title = Gtk.Label(label="Аппарат и заметки", xalign=0)
        identity_title.add_css_class("heading")
        super().__init__(win, test_title, checks, identity_title, identity_box,
                         self.run_button, self.continue_button, self.finish_partial_button,
                         self.save_button, help_text)
        self._results = {}
        self._context = None
        self._selected_order = []
        self._parameters = {}
        self._started = None

    def _snapshot(self):
        response = self.win.response_page
        thd = self.win.thd_page
        noise = self.win.noise_page
        imd = self.win.imd_page
        mode = ("flat", "riaa")[response.mode.get_selected()]
        target = response.iec.get_selected()
        if self.chk_response.get_active() and mode == "riaa" and target == 0:
            raise ValueError("Для Inverse-RIAA выбери эталон RIAA или RIAA + IEC")
        if self.chk_response.get_active():
            profile, profile_note = self.win.get_response_calibration(self.win.rig_fs())
        else:
            profile, profile_note = None, None
        self._response_profile = profile
        self._response_profile_note = profile_note
        return {
            "response": {
                "f_lo": float(response.lo.get_value()), "f_hi": float(response.hi.get_value()),
                "dur": float(response.dur.get_value()),
                "level_1k_dbfs": float(response.lv.get_value()), "mode": mode,
                "iec": target == 2, "calibration_note": profile_note,
            },
            "thd": {"frequency_hz": float(thd.f.get_value()),
                    "level_dbfs": float(thd.lv.get_value()),
                    "route": ("both", "L", "R")[thd.route.get_selected()]},
            "noise": {"seconds": float(noise.sec.get_value())},
            "imd": {"f1_hz": float(imd.f1.get_value()), "f2_hz": float(imd.f2.get_value()),
                    "mode": ("ccif", "smpte")[imd.mode.get_selected()],
                    "level_dbfs": float(imd.lv.get_value())},
        }

    def run_batch(self):
        if self.win.busy:
            self.win.toast("Другое измерение ещё выполняется")
            return
        selected = [key for key, widget in (
            ("response", self.chk_response), ("thd", self.chk_thd),
            ("noise", self.chk_noise), ("imd", self.chk_imd)) if widget.get_active()]
        if not selected:
            self.win.toast("Выбери хотя бы один тест")
            return
        device_id = self.win.measurement_device_id.get_text().strip()
        if not device_id:
            self.win.toast("Укажи номер аппарата, например 590")
            return
        try:
            self._parameters = self._snapshot()
        except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
            self.win.toast(str(exc))
            return
        self._selected_order = (["noise"] if "noise" in selected else []) + [
            key for key in ("response", "thd", "imd") if key in selected]
        self._results = {}
        self._context = None
        self._started = time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z")
        self.continue_button.set_visible(False)
        self.save_button.set_sensitive(False)
        self.run_button.set_sensitive(False)
        if "noise" in selected:
            seconds = self._parameters["noise"]["seconds"]
            self.win.run_job(lambda rig: m.noise(rig, seconds), self._noise_done,
                             "Пакет 1/{} · измерение собственного шума".format(len(self._selected_order)),
                             clock_sensitive=False)
        else:
            self.run_driven_tests()

    def _noise_done(self, result):
        self._context = result.get("_valvebench_context")
        self._results["noise"] = {k: v for k, v in result.items() if k != "_valvebench_context"}
        driven = [k for k in ("response", "thd", "imd") if k in self._selected_order]
        if driven:
            self.continue_button.set_visible(True)
            self.finish_partial_button.set_visible(True)
            self.say("Шум записан. Теперь отключи терминирующий резистор/замыкание входа DUT, "
                     "подключи Anti-RIAA и нажми «ПОДКЛЮЧИЛ ANTI-RIAA · ПРОДОЛЖИТЬ».\n\n"
                     "Шум уже сохранён в памяти пакета и войдёт в общий паспорт.")
        else:
            self.run_button.set_sensitive(True)
            self._finalize("complete")

    def finish_partial(self):
        if self._results:
            self._finalize("partial")

    def run_driven_tests(self):
        if self._context:
            expected = self._context.get("device", {}).get("stable_id")
            current_id = self.win.audio_pair_key()
            expected_fs = int(self._context.get("audio_io", {}).get("sample_rate_hz", 0) or 0)
            if (expected and current_id != expected) or (expected_fs and int(self.win.rig_fs()) != expected_fs):
                self.continue_button.set_visible(True)
                self.win.toast("Верни исходную звуковую карту и частоту дискретизации пакета")
                return
        self.continue_button.set_visible(False)
        self.finish_partial_button.set_visible(False)
        self.run_button.set_sensitive(False)
        tests = [k for k in ("response", "thd", "imd") if k in self._selected_order and k not in self._results]
        if not tests:
            self._finalize("complete")
            return
        params = self._parameters
        profile, profile_note = getattr(self, "_response_profile", None), getattr(self, "_response_profile_note", None)

        def job(rig):
            expected_mixer = ((self._context or {}).get("audio_io") or {}).get("alsa_mixer_fingerprint")
            dac_hw = str(getattr(rig.be, "hw_out", getattr(rig.be, "hw", "sim")))
            adc_hw = str(getattr(rig.be, "hw_in", getattr(rig.be, "hw", "sim")))
            if expected_mixer:
                actual_mixer = Window._mixer_fingerprint_for_pair(dac_hw, adc_hw)
                if actual_mixer != expected_mixer:
                    raise m.MeasureError(
                        "Микшер изменился после измерения шума. Верни прежние уровни/маршрутизацию "
                        "и начни пакет заново, иначе результаты будут несопоставимы.")
            output = {"kind": "dut_batch_driven_phase", "tests": {}, "test_order": []}
            for index, key in enumerate(tests, 1):
                GLib.idle_add(self._set_progress, f"Пакет: {key.upper()} · {index}/{len(tests)}")
                try:
                    if key == "response":
                        a = {k: v for k, v in params["response"].items() if k != "calibration_note"}
                        value = m.sweep(rig, response_calibration=profile, **a)
                        if profile_note:
                            value["calibration_note"] = profile_note
                    elif key == "thd":
                        a = params["thd"]
                        value = m.thd_point(rig, a["frequency_hz"], a["level_dbfs"], a["route"])
                    else:
                        a = params["imd"]
                        value = m.imd(rig, a["f1_hz"], a["f2_hz"], a["mode"], a["level_dbfs"])
                    output["tests"][key] = value
                    output["test_order"].append(key)
                except Exception as exc:  # keep successful neighbouring tests
                    output["tests"][key] = {"status": "error", "error_type": type(exc).__name__,
                                            "error": str(exc)}
                    output["test_order"].append(key)
            return output

        self.win.run_job(job, self._driven_done, "Пакет: выполняю АЧХ, THD и IMD по очереди…")

    def _set_progress(self, text):
        self.job_status.set_text(text)
        return False

    def _driven_done(self, phase):
        if self._context is None:
            self._context = phase.get("_valvebench_context")
        for key, value in phase.get("tests", {}).items():
            self._results[key] = value
        self._finalize("complete" if all(v.get("kind") for v in self._results.values()) else "partial")

    def on_job_error(self, exc):
        self.run_button.set_sensitive(True)
        if self._results:
            self._finalize("partial")
        else:
            self.say(f"Пакет не завершён: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")

    def _finalize(self, state):
        bundle = {
            "kind": "dut_batch", "schema": 1, "state": state,
            "started_at_local": self._started,
            "finished_at_local": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z"),
            "test_order": [k for k in self._selected_order if k in self._results],
            "parameters": self._parameters,
            "tests": self._results,
        }
        if self._context:
            bundle["_valvebench_context"] = self._context
        self.win.last = bundle
        self.win._update_export_button()
        self.save_button.set_sensitive(bool(self.win.export_measurement_btn.get_sensitive()))
        self.run_button.set_sensitive(True)
        self.continue_button.set_visible(False)
        self.finish_partial_button.set_visible(False)
        lines = [f"ПАКЕТ {state.upper()} · {len(self._results)} тест(а)"]
        for key in bundle["test_order"]:
            result = self._results[key]
            if result.get("status") == "error":
                lines.append(f"{key.upper()}: ОШИБКА · {result.get('error')}")
            else:
                lines.append(f"{key.upper()}: {result.get('kind', 'готово')}")
                try:
                    renderer = {"noise": m.text_noise, "response": m.text_sweep,
                                "thd": m.text_thd, "imd": m.text_imd}.get(key)
                    if renderer:
                        lines.append(renderer(result))
                except Exception:
                    pass
        if not self.win.measurement_device_id.get_text().strip():
            lines.append("Укажи ID аппарата для сохранения всего пакета.")
        else:
            lines.append("Все результаты сохраняются одной кнопкой в один паспорт устройства.")
        self.say("\n\n".join(lines))

        for w in (self.f, self.lv):
            w.connect("notify::value", lambda *_: self.apply())
        self.route.connect("notify::selected", lambda *_: self.apply())
        self.series_level.connect("notify::value", lambda *_: self.refresh_gain_reference())
        self.series_profile.connect("notify::selected", lambda *_: self.refresh_gain_reference())
        self.series_riaa.connect("notify::active", lambda *_: self.refresh_gain_reference())
        self.refresh_series_freqs_label()

    def series_frequencies(self):
        """(имя профиля, кортеж частот) по выбору пользователя."""
        name = list(gainmatch.FREQUENCY_PROFILES)[max(0, self.series_profile.get_selected())]
        return name, gainmatch.FREQUENCY_PROFILES[name][1]

    def refresh_series_freqs_label(self):
        _, freqs = self.series_frequencies()
        mains = float(self.win.cal.get("mains", 50.0) or 50.0)
        self.series_freqs_label.set_text("Фактические частоты: " +
                                         " / ".join(f"{f:g}" for f in freqs) + " Гц"
                                         + f" · проверка близости к сети: {mains:g} Гц")
        if self.series_profile.get_selected() == 1:
            self.series_freqs_label.set_text(self.series_freqs_label.get_text() +
                                             " · обход гармоник сети 50 Гц")
        self.refresh_gain_reference()

    def set_job_busy(self, busy):
        if busy:
            if self._job_button_states is None:
                self._job_button_states = [(w, w.get_sensitive()) for w in Page._walk_widgets(self)
                                           if isinstance(w, Gtk.Button)]
                for widget, _state in self._job_button_states:
                    widget.set_sensitive(False)
        elif self._job_button_states is not None:
            for widget, was_sensitive in self._job_button_states:
                widget.set_sensitive(was_sensitive)
            self._job_button_states = None

    def set_job_status(self, state, message, elapsed=None):
        if state == "running":
            self.series_job_spinner.start()
            text = f"ИЗМЕРЕНИЕ ВЫПОЛНЯЕТСЯ · {message}"
        else:
            self.series_job_spinner.stop()
            if state == "success":
                suffix = f" · {elapsed:.1f} с" if elapsed is not None else ""
                text = f"ИЗМЕРЕНИЕ ЗАВЕРШЕНО{suffix} · результат ниже"
            elif state == "error":
                text = f"ИЗМЕРЕНИЕ НЕ ВЫПОЛНЕНО · {message}"
            else:
                text = str(message)
        self.series_job_status.set_text(text)
        self.series_job_status.set_tooltip_text(text)

    def refresh_gain_reference(self):
        """Invalidate the in-session reference when card, rate or drive level changes.

        A saved file is kept for traceability, but never silently reused after app
        restart: USB mixer defaults can change on reconnect.
        """
        hw = self.win.selected_adc_hw()
        key = self.win.audio_pair_key()
        level = float(self.series_level.get_value())
        _, freqs = self.series_frequencies()
        riaa = self.series_riaa.get_active()
        ref = self.series_reference
        matches = bool(ref and ref.get("device_key") == key
                       and int(ref.get("fs", -1)) == int(self.win.rig_fs())
                       and abs(float(ref.get("generator_level_dbfs", 999.0)) - level) < 1e-6
                       and bool(ref.get("riaa_compensated", False)) == bool(riaa)
                       and {round(float(f), 6) for f in ref.get("frequencies_hz", [])}
                       == {round(float(f), 6) for f in freqs})
        if not matches:
            self.series_reference = None
            self.series_reference_label.set_text("Сначала проверь измерительную карту (шаг 1)")
            self.series_dut_btn.set_sensitive(False)
        else:
            self.series_reference_label.set_text(
                f"КОРРЕКЦИЯ КАРТЫ ГОТОВА · {ref['fs']} Гц · можно подключать усилитель")
            self.series_dut_btn.set_sensitive(True)

    def _series_metadata(self):
        return {key: value for key, value in {
            "dut": self.series_dut.get_text().strip(),
            "tubes": self.series_tubes.get_text().strip(),
            "notes": self.series_notes.get_text().strip(),
        }.items() if value}

    def measure_loopback(self):
        """Step 1: characterize the soundcard's L/R loop imbalance per frequency."""
        self.series_reference = None
        self.series_dut_btn.set_sensitive(False)
        self.series_reference_label.set_text("Эталон петли: измеряю…")
        level = float(self.series_level.get_value())
        repeats = int(self.series_repeats.get_value())
        profile, freqs = self.series_frequencies()
        riaa = self.series_riaa.get_active()

        def job(rig):
            return gainmatch.measure_series(rig, frequencies=freqs, level_dbfs=level,
                                            repeats=repeats, purpose="loopback",
                                            riaa_compensate=riaa,
                                            frequency_profile=profile)

        def done(result):
            try:
                reference = gainmatch.build_loopback_reference(result)
                path = store.save_gain_reference(reference)
            except (ValueError, OSError):
                self.series_reference = None
                self.series_dut_btn.set_sensitive(False)
                self.refresh_gain_reference()
                raise
